+function splitDataFor(splits: DashboardExpenseSplitRow[] | null | undefined, memberAliases: Map<string, MemberId>) {
+  const splitRates: Partial<SplitRates> = {};
+
+  for (const split of splits ?? []) {
+    const alias = memberAliases.get(split.member_id);
+    const rate = Number(split.rate);
+    if (alias && Number.isFinite(rate)) {
+      splitRates[alias] = rate;
+    }
+  }
+
+  if (splitRates.a === undefined || splitRates.b === undefined) {
+    return {};
+  }
+
+  let splitPreset: SplitPreset | undefined;
+  if (splitRates.a === 0.5 && splitRates.b === 0.5) splitPreset = "equal";
+  if (splitRates.a === 0.7 && splitRates.b === 0.3) splitPreset = "a70";
+  if (splitRates.a === 0.3 && splitRates.b === 0.7) splitPreset = "b70";
+
+  return {
+    splitPreset,
+    splitRates: splitRates as SplitRates,
+  };

*** Add File: src/app/api/expenses/[id]/route.ts
+import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
+import { db } from "../../../../lib/db";
+import type { ExpenseScope, SplitPreset } from "../../../../lib/expense-utils";
+
+export const runtime = "nodejs";
+export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
+
+type UpdateExpenseRequest = {
+  scope?: unknown;
+  splitPreset?: unknown;
+};
+
+type HouseholdRow = { id: string };
+type MemberRow = { id: string };
+
+const householdQuery = `
+  select id
+  from households
+  order by created_at asc
+  limit 1
+`;
+
+const membersQuery = `
+  select id
+  from household_members
+  where household_id = $1
+  order by role asc, created_at asc
+`;
+
+const expenseQuery = `
+  select id
+  from expenses
+  where id = $1 and household_id = $2
+`;
+
+const updateExpenseQuery = `
+  update expenses
+  set scope = $2
+  where id = $1
+`;
+
+const deleteSplitsQuery = `
+  delete from expense_splits
+  where expense_id = $1
+`;
+
+const insertSplitQuery = `
+  insert into expense_splits (expense_id, member_id, rate)
+  values ($1, $2, $3)
+`;
+
+function parseScope(value: unknown): ExpenseScope | null {
+  if (value === "shared" || value === "personal" || value === "review") return value;
+  return null;
+}
+
+function parseSplitPreset(value: unknown): SplitPreset {
+  if (value === "a70" || value === "b70") return value;
+  return "equal";
+}
+
+function splitRatesFor(preset: SplitPreset) {
+  if (preset === "a70") return [0.7, 0.3];
+  if (preset === "b70") return [0.3, 0.7];
+  return [0.5, 0.5];
+}
+
+export async function PATCH(request: Request, context: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
+  try {
+    const { id } = await context.params;
+    const body = (await request.json()) as UpdateExpenseRequest;
+    const scope = parseScope(body.scope);
+    const splitPreset = parseSplitPreset(body.splitPreset);
+
+    if (!scope) {
+      return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_expense_update" }, { status: 400 });
+    }
+
+    const pool = db();
+    const client = await pool.connect();
+
+    try {
+      await client.query("begin");
+
+      const household = (await client.query<HouseholdRow>(householdQuery)).rows[0];
+      if (!household) {
+        await client.query("rollback");
+        return NextResponse.json({ error: "household_not_found" }, { status: 404 });
+      }
+
+      const expense = (await client.query(expenseQuery, [id, household.id])).rows[0];
+      if (!expense) {
+        await client.query("rollback");
+        return NextResponse.json({ error: "expense_not_found" }, { status: 404 });
+      }
+
+      await client.query(updateExpenseQuery, [id, scope]);
+      await client.query(deleteSplitsQuery, [id]);
+
+      if (scope === "shared") {
+        const members = (await client.query<MemberRow>(membersQuery, [household.id])).rows;
+        const rates = splitRatesFor(splitPreset);
+
+        if (members.length !== rates.length) {
+          await client.query("rollback");
+          return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_household_members" }, { status: 400 });
+        }
+
+        for (const [index, member] of members.entries()) {
+          await client.query(insertSplitQuery, [id, member.id, rates[index]]);
+        }
+      }
+
+      await client.query("commit");
+      return NextResponse.json({
+        expense: {
+          id,
+          scope,
+          splitPreset: scope === "shared" ? splitPreset : undefined,
+        },
+      });
+    } catch (error) {
+      await client.query("rollback");
+      throw error;
+    } finally {
+      client.release();
+    }
+  } catch (error) {
+    console.error("Failed to update expense", error);
+    return NextResponse.json({ error: "expense_update_failed" }, { status: 500 });
+  }
+}
*** End Patch

from pathlib import Path

root = Path('/home/haya-ryoo/apps/kakeibo')

def replace(path, old, new):
    file = root / path
    text = file.read_text()
    if old not in text:
        raise SystemExit(f'missing expected text in {path}: {old[:80]!r}')
    file.write_text(text.replace(old, new, 1))

'''export function splitRatesForPreset(preset: SplitPreset | undefined): SplitRates {\n  if (preset === "a70") {\n    return { a: 0.7, b: 0.3 };\n  }\n\n  if (preset === "b70") {\n    return { a: 0.3, b: 0.7 };\n  }\n\n  return { a: 0.5, b: 0.5 };\n}\n\nfunction sharesFor(expense: Expense) {\n  const rates = expense.splitRates ?? splitRatesForPreset(expense.splitPreset);\n\n  return [\n    { memberId: "a", rate: rates.a },\n    { memberId: "b", rate: rates.b },\n  ];\n}\n''')

'''function splitDataFor(splits: DashboardExpenseSplitRow[] | null | undefined, memberAliases: Map<string, MemberId>) {\n  const splitRates: Partial<SplitRates> = {};\n\n  for (const split of splits ?? []) {\n    const alias = memberAliases.get(split.member_id);\n    const rate = Number(split.rate);\n    if (alias && Number.isFinite(rate)) {\n      splitRates[alias] = rate;\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (splitRates.a === undefined || splitRates.b === undefined) {\n    return {};\n  }\n\n  let splitPreset: SplitPreset | undefined;\n  if (splitRates.a === 0.5 && splitRates.b === 0.5) splitPreset = "equal";\n  if (splitRates.a === 0.7 && splitRates.b === 0.3) splitPreset = "a70";\n  if (splitRates.a === 0.3 && splitRates.b === 0.7) splitPreset = "b70";\n\n  return {\n    splitPreset,\n    splitRates: splitRates as SplitRates,\n  };\n}\n\nexport function mapDashboardExpenses(memberRows: DashboardMemberRow[], expenseRows: DashboardExpenseRow[]): Expense[] {\n''')
replace('src/features/dashboard/dashboard.mapper.ts',

(memberAliases.get(expense.paid_by_member_id) ?? "a") : "a",\n    scope: expense.scope,\n    category: expense.category,\n    note: noteFor(expense.title, expense.memo),\n    createdAt: isoDateTime(expense.created_at ?? expense.expense_date),\n  }));\n''',
'''  return expenseRows.map((expense) => {\n    const splitData = splitDataFor(expense.splits, memberAliases);\n\n    return {\n      id: expense.id,\n      title: expense.title,\n      amount: expense.amount,\n      paidBy: expense.paid_by_member_id ? (memberAliases.get(expense.paid_by_member_id) ?? "a") : "a",\n      scope: expense.scope,\n      category: expense.category,\n      note: noteFor(expense.title, expense.memo),\n      createdAt: isoDateTime(expense.created_at ?? expense.expense_date),\n      ...splitData,\n    };\n  });\n''')

file.write_text('''import { NextResponse } from "next/server";\nimport { db } from "../../../../lib/db";\nimport type { ExpenseScope, SplitPreset } from "../../../../lib/expense-utils";\n\nexport const runtime = "nodejs";\nexport const dynamic = "force-dynamic";\n\ntype UpdateExpenseRequest = {\n  scope?: unknown;\n  splitPreset?: unknown;\n};\n\ntype HouseholdRow = { id: string };\ntype MemberRow = { id: string };\n\nconst householdQuery = `\n  select id\n  from households\n  order by created_at asc\n  limit 1\n`;\n\nconst membersQuery = `\n  select id\n  from household_members\n  where household_id = $1\n  order by role asc, created_at asc\n`;\n\nconst expenseQuery = `\n  select id\n  from expenses\n  where id = $1 and household_id = $2\n`;\n\nconst updateExpenseQuery = `\n  update expenses\n  set scope = $2\n  where id = $1\n`;\n\nconst deleteSplitsQuery = `\n  delete from expense_splits\n  where expense_id = $1\n`;\n\nconst insertSplitQuery = `\n  insert into expense_splits (expense_id, member_id, rate)\n  values ($1, $2, $3)\n`;\n\nfunction parseScope(value: unknown): ExpenseScope | null {\n  if (value === "shared" || value === "personal" || value === "review") return value;\n  return null;\n}\n\nfunction parseSplitPreset(value: unknown): SplitPreset {\n  if (value === "a70" || value === "b70") return value;\n  return "equal";\n}\n\nfunction splitRatesFor(preset: SplitPreset) {\n  if (preset === "a70") return [0.7, 0.3];\n  if (preset === "b70") return [0.3, 0.7];\n  return [0.5, 0.5];\n}\n\nexport async function PATCH(request: Request, context: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {\n  try {\n    const { id } = await context.params;\n    const body = (await request.json()) as UpdateExpenseRequest;\n    const scope = parseScope(body.scope);\n    const splitPreset = parseSplitPreset(body.splitPreset);\n\n    if (!scope) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_expense_update" }, { status: 400 });\n    }\n\n    const pool = db();\n    const client = await pool.connect();\n\n    try {\n      await client.query("begin");\n\n      const household = (await client.query<HouseholdRow>(householdQuery)).rows[0];\n      if (!household) {\n        await client.query("rollback");\n        return NextResponse.json({ error: "household_not_found" }, { status: 404 });\n      }\n\n      const expense = (await client.query(expenseQuery, [id, household.id])).rows[0];\n      if (!expense) {\n        await client.query("rollback");\n        return NextResponse.json({ error: "expense_not_found" }, { status: 404 });\n      }\n\n      await client.query(updateExpenseQuery, [id, scope]);\n      await client.query(deleteSplitsQuery, [id]);\n\n      if (scope === "shared") {\n        const members = (await client.query<MemberRow>(membersQuery, [household.id])).rows;\n        const rates = splitRatesFor(splitPreset);\n\n        if (members.length !== rates.length) {\n          await client.query("rollback");\n          return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_household_members" }, { status: 400 });\n        }\n\n        for (const [index, member] of members.entries()) {\n          await client.query(insertSplitQuery, [id, member.id, rates[index]]);\n        }\n      }\n\n      await client.query("commit");\n      return NextResponse.json({\n        expense: {\n          id,\n          scope,\n          splitPreset: scope === "shared" ? splitPreset : undefined,\n        },\n      });\n    } catch (error) {\n      await client.query("rollback");\n      throw error;\n    } finally {\n      client.release();\n    }\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error("Failed to update expense", error);\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: "expense_update_failed" }, { status: 500 });\n  }\n}\n''')
PY

from pathlib import Path
root = Path('/home/haya-ryoo/apps/kakeibo')

def replace(path, old, new):
    file = root / path
    text = file.read_text()
    if old not in text:
        raise SystemExit(f'missing expected text in {path}: {old[:100]!r}')
    file.write_text(text.replace(old, new, 1))

from pathlib import Path
root = Path('/home/haya-ryoo/apps/kakeibo')

(root / 'src/app/api/dashboard/route.ts').write_text('''import { NextResponse } from "next/server";\nimport { db } from "../../../lib/db";\nimport { mapDashboardExpenses, type DashboardExpenseRow, type DashboardMemberRow } from "../../../features/dashboard/dashboard.mapper";\n\nexport const runtime = "nodejs";\nexport const dynamic = "force-dynamic";\n\ntype SettlementPeriodStatus = "open" | "reviewing" | "settled";\n\nconst householdQuery = `\n  select id\n  from households\n  order by created_at asc\n  limit 1\n`;\n\nconst membersQuery = `\n  select id, display_name\n  from household_members\n  where household_id = $1\n  order by role asc, created_at asc\n`;\n\nconst expensesQuery = `\n  select\n    e.id::text,\n    case when c.kind = 'rent' then c.name else coalesce(nullif(split_part(e.memo, ' ', 1), ''), c.name) end as title,\n    e.amount,\n    e.paid_by_member_id::text,\n    e.scope,\n    c.name as category,\n    e.memo,\n    e.expense_date::text,\n    e.expense_date::text as created_at,\n    coalesce((\n      select json_agg(\n        json_build_object(\n          'member_id', es.member_id::text,\n          'rate', es.rate::float8\n        )\n        order by hm.role asc, hm.created_at asc\n      )\n      from expense_splits es\n      join household_members hm on hm.id = es.member_id\n      where es.expense_id = e.id and es.rate is not null\n    ), '[]'::json) as splits\n  from expenses e\n  join categories c on c.id = e.category_id\n  where e.household_id = $1\n    and e.expense_date >= make_date($2, $3, 1)\n    and e.expense_date < make_date($2, $3, 1) + interval '1 month'\n  order by e.expense_date desc, e.created_at desc\n`;\n\nconst settlementPeriodQuery = `\n  select status\n  from settlement_periods\n  where household_id = $1 and year = $2 and month = $3\n`;\n\nfunction parsePeriod(request: Request) {\n  const url = new URL(request.url);\n  const now = new Date();\n  const year = Number(url.searchParams.get("year") ?? now.getFullYear());\n  const month = Number(url.searchParams.get("month") ?? now.getMonth() + 1);\n\n  if (!Number.isInteger(year) || year < 2000 || year > 9999 || !Number.isInteger(month) || month < 1 || month > 12) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  return { year, month };\n}\n\nexport async function GET(request: Request) {\n  try {\n    const period = parsePeriod(request);\n    if (!period) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_period" }, { status: 400 });\n    }\n\n    const pool = db();\n    const householdResult = await pool.query<{ id: string }>(householdQuery);\n    const household = householdResult.rows[0];\n\n    if (!household) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ members: [], expenses: [], period: { ...period, status: "open" } });\n    }\n\n    const [memberResult, expenseResult, settlementPeriodResult] = await Promise.all([\n      pool.query<DashboardMemberRow>(membersQuery, [household.id]),\n      pool.query<DashboardExpenseRow>(expensesQuery, [household.id, period.year, period.month]),\n      pool.query<{ status: SettlementPeriodStatus }>(settlementPeriodQuery, [household.id, period.year, period.month]),\n    ]);\n\n    return NextResponse.json({\n      members: memberResult.rows,\n      expenses: mapDashboardExpenses(memberResult.rows, expenseResult.rows),\n      period: {\n        ...period,\n        status: settlementPeriodResult.rows[0]?.status ?? "open",\n      },\n    });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error("Failed to load dashboard data", error);\n\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: "dashboard_load_failed" },\n      { status: 500 },\n    );\n  }\n}\n''')

"../../../features/settlement/settlement.types";\nimport { db } from "../../../lib/db";\n\nexport const runtime = "nodejs";\nexport const dynamic = "force-dynamic";\n\ntype CreateSettlementRequest = {\n  year?: unknown;\n  month?: unknown;\n};\n\ntype HouseholdRow = { id: string };\ntype MemberRow = { id: string; display_name: string };\ntype ExpenseRow = {\n  id: string;\n  amount: number;\n  paid_by_member_id: string | null;\n  scope: SettlementExpense["scope"];\n  splits: { member_id: string; rate: number | string }[];\n};\n\nconst householdQuery = `\n  select id\n  from households\n  order by created_at asc\n  limit 1\n`;\n\nconst membersQuery = `\n  select id::text, display_name\n  from household_members\n  where household_id = $1\n  order by role asc, created_at asc\n`;\n\nconst expensesQuery = `\n  select\n    e.id::text,\n    e.amount,\n    e.paid_by_member_id::text,\n    e.scope,\n    coalesce((\n      select json_agg(\n        json_build_object(\n          'member_id', es.member_id::text,\n          'rate', es.rate::float8\n        )\n        order by hm.role asc, hm.created_at asc\n      )\n      from expense_splits es\n      join household_members hm on hm.id = es.member_id\n      where es.expense_id = e.id and es.rate is not null\n    ), '[]'::json) as splits\n  from expenses e\n  where e.household_id = $1\n    and e.expense_date >= make_date($2, $3, 1)\n    and e.expense_date < make_date($2, $3, 1) + interval '1 month'\n  order by e.expense_date asc, e.created_at asc\n`;\n\nconst upsertPeriodQuery = `\n  insert into settlement_periods (household_id, year, month, status, settled_at)\n  values ($1, $2, $3, 'settled', now())\n  on conflict (household_id, year, month) do update\n  set status = 'settled', settled_at = now()\n  returning id::text\n`;\n\nconst deleteSettlementsQuery = `\n  delete from settlements\n  where period_id = $1\n`;\n\nconst insertSettlementQuery = `\n  insert into settlements (period_id, from_member_id, to_member_id, amount, status)\n  values ($1, $2, $3, $4, 'pending')\n`;\n\nfunction parsePeriod(body: CreateSettlementRequest) {\n  const year = typeof body.year === "number" ? body.year : Number.NaN;\n  const month = typeof body.month === "number" ? body.month : Number.NaN;\n\n  if (!Number.isInteger(year) || year < 2000 || year > 9999 || !Number.isInteger(month) || month < 1 || month > 12) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  return { year, month };\n}\n\nfunction settlementExpenses(rows: ExpenseRow[]): SettlementExpense[] {\n  return rows.map((expense) => ({\n    id: expense.id,\n    amount: expense.amount,\n    paidByMemberId: expense.paid_by_member_id,\n    scope: expense.scope,\n    shares: expense.splits.map((split) => ({\n      memberId: split.member_id,\n      rate: Number(split.rate),\n    })),\n  }));\n}\n\nexport async function POST(request: Request) {\n  try {\n    const period = parsePeriod((await request.json()) as CreateSettlementRequest);\n    if (!period) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_period" }, { status: 400 });\n    }\n\n    const pool = db();\n    const client = await pool.connect();\n\n    try {\n      await client.query("begin");\n\n      const household = (await client.query<HouseholdRow>(householdQuery)).rows[0];\n      if (!household) {\n        await client.query("rollback");\n        return NextResponse.json({ error: "household_not_found" }, { status: 404 });\n      }\n\n      const [memberResult, expenseResult] = await Promise.all([\n        client.query<MemberRow>(membersQuery, [household.id]),\n        client.query<ExpenseRow>(expensesQuery, [household.id, period.year, period.month]),\n      ]);\n      const summary = calculateSettlement(\n        memberResult.rows.map((member) => ({ id: member.id, name: member.display_name })),\n        settlementExpenses(expenseResult.rows),\n      );\n\n      if (summary.reviewCount > 0) {\n        await client.query("rollback");\n        return NextResponse.json({ error: "review_expenses_remaining", reviewCount: summary.reviewCount }, { status: 409 });\n      }\n\n      const settlementPeriod = (await client.query<{ id: string }>(upsertPeriodQuery, [household.id, period.year, period.month])).rows[0];\n      await client.query(deleteSettlementsQuery, [settlementPeriod.id]);\n\n      for (const transfer of summary.transfers) {\n        await client.query(insertSettlementQuery, [settlementPeriod.id, transfer.fromMemberId, transfer.toMemberId, transfer.amount]);\n      }\n\n      await client.query("commit");\n      return NextResponse.json({ period: { ...period, status: "settled" }, summary }, { status: 201 });\n    } catch (error) {\n      await client.query("rollback");\n      throw error;\n    } finally {\n      client.release();\n    }\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error("Failed to settle period", error);\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: "settlement_create_failed" }, { status: 500 });\n  }\n}\n''')
PY

    text = text.replace(old, new, 1)

replace('''import { Camera, Check, Home, List, Plus, ReceiptText, Send, Settings, Wallet } from "lucide-react";\n''', '''import { Camera, Check, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Home, List, Plus, ReceiptText, Send, Settings, Wallet } from "lucide-react";\n''')
replace('''type DashboardResponse = {\n  expenses: Expense[];\n};\n''', '''type SettlementPeriodStatus = "open" | "reviewing" | "settled";\n\ntype Period = {\n  year: number;\n  month: number;\n};\n\ntype DashboardResponse = {\n  expenses: Expense[];\n  period: Period & { status: SettlementPeriodStatus };\n};\n''')
replace('''function splitLabelFor(expense: Expense) {\n''', '''function currentPeriod(): Period {\n  const now = new Date();\n  return { year: now.getFullYear(), month: now.getMonth() + 1 };\n}\n\nfunction movePeriod(period: Period, offset: number): Period {\n  const date = new Date(period.year, period.month - 1 + offset, 1);\n  return { year: date.getFullYear(), month: date.getMonth() + 1 };\n}\n\nfunction periodLabel(period: Period) {\n  return `${period.year}年${period.month}月`;\n}\n\nfunction splitLabelFor(expense: Expense) {\n''')
replace('''  const [expenses, setExpenses] = useState<Expense[]>(initialExpenses);\n  const [saveError, setSaveError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n''', '''  const [expenses, setExpenses] = useState<Expense[]>(initialExpenses);\n  const [selectedPeriod, setSelectedPeriod] = useState<Period>(currentPeriod);\n  const [periodStatus, setPeriodStatus] = useState<SettlementPeriodStatus>("open");\n  const [saveError, setSaveError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [isSettling, setIsSettling] = useState(false);\n''')
replace('''        const response = await fetch("/api/dashboard");\n        if (!response.ok) return;\n\n        const data = (await response.json()) as DashboardResponse;\n        if (active && data.expenses.length > 0) {\n          setExpenses(data.expenses);\n        }\n''', '''        const response = await fetch(`/api/dashboard?year=${selectedPeriod.year}&month=${selectedPeriod.month}`);\n        if (!response.ok) return;\n\n        const data = (await response.json()) as DashboardResponse;\n        if (active) {\n          setExpenses(data.expenses);\n          setPeriodStatus(data.period.status);\n        }\n''')
replace('''  }, []);\n\n  const stats = summaryFor(expenses);\n''', '''  }, [selectedPeriod]);\n\n  const stats = summaryFor(expenses);\n  const nowPeriod = currentPeriod();\n  const isCurrentPeriod = selectedPeriod.year === nowPeriod.year && selectedPeriod.month === nowPeriod.month;\n''')
replace('''  const addExpense = async (title: string) => {\n    const draft = parseExpenseInput(title, selectedPayer);\n''', '''  const addExpense = async (title: string) => {\n    if (!isCurrentPeriod) {\n      setSaveError("過去または未来の月を表示中です。支出追加は今月へ戻ってから行ってください。");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const draft = parseExpenseInput(title, selectedPayer);\n''')
replace('''  const expenseCard = (expense: Expense, options?: { showSplitPicker?: boolean }) => (\n''', '''  const settlePeriod = async () => {\n    setSaveError(null);\n    setIsSettling(true);\n\n    try {\n      const response = await fetch("/api/settlements", {\n        method: "POST",\n        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n        body: JSON.stringify(selectedPeriod),\n      });\n\n      if (!response.ok) throw new Error("settlement_create_failed");\n      setPeriodStatus("settled");\n    } catch {\n      setSaveError("精算を確定できませんでした。要確認の支出と通信状態を確認してください。");\n    } finally {\n      setIsSettling(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const expenseCard = (expense: Expense, options?: { showSplitPicker?: boolean }) => (\n''')
replace('''        <button className="action-button" type="button" disabled={stats.reviewCount > 0}>\n          精算済みにする\n        </button>\n''', '''        <button className="action-button" type="button" disabled={stats.reviewCount > 0 || periodStatus === "settled" || isSettling} onClick={() => void settlePeriod()}>\n          {periodStatus === "settled" ? "精算済み" : isSettling ? "確定中..." : "精算済みにする"}\n        </button>\n''')
replace('''              <div className="eyebrow">\n                <Wallet size={14} />\n                2026年5月\n              </div>\n''', '''              <div className="period-switcher">\n                <button className="period-switcher__button" type="button" aria-label="前月を見る" onClick={() => setSelectedPeriod((period) => movePeriod(period, -1))}>\n                  <ChevronLeft size={16} />\n                </button>\n                <div className="eyebrow">\n                  <Wallet size={14} />\n                  {periodLabel(selectedPeriod)}\n                </div>\n                <button className="period-switcher__button" type="button" aria-label="翌月を見る" onClick={() => setSelectedPeriod((period) => movePeriod(period, 1))}>\n                  <ChevronRight size={16} />\n                </button>\n              </div>\n''')
replace('''            value={composerValue}\n            onChange={(event) => setComposerValue(event.target.value)}\n''', '''            value={composerValue}\n            disabled={!isCurrentPeriod}\n            onChange={(event) => setComposerValue(event.target.value)}\n''')

replace('''            <button key={item} className="chip" type="button" onClick={() => setComposerValue(item)}>\n''', '''            <button key={item} className="chip" type="button" disabled={!isCurrentPeriod} onClick={() => setComposerValue(item)}>\n''')

}>("select rate::text from expense_splits where expense_id = $1 order by rate desc", [testExpenseId]);\n    assert.deepEqual(rows.map((row) => row.rate), ["0.7000", "0.3000"]);\n  });\n\n  it("rejects await import("../../src/app/api/settlements/route.ts");\n\n    await PATCH(\n      new Request(`http://localhost/api/expenses/${testExpenseId}`, {\n        method: "PATCH",\n        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n        body: JSON.stringify({ scope: "review" }),\n      }),\n      { params: Promise.resolve({ id: testExpenseId }) },\n    );\n    const blocked = await POST(new Request("http://localhost/api/settlements", {\n      method: "POST",\n      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n      body: JSON.stringify(testPeriod),\n    }));\n    assert.equal(blocked.status, 409);\n\n    await PATCH(\n      new Request(`http://localhost/api/expenses/${testExpenseId}`, {\n        method: "PATCH",\n        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n        body: JSON.stringify({ scope: "shared", splitPreset: "equal" }),\n      }),\n      { params: Promise.resolve({ id: testExpenseId }) },\n    );\n    const settled = await POST(new Request("http://localhost/api/settlements", {\n      method: "POST",\n      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n      body: JSON.stringify(testPeriod),\n    }));\n    assert.equal(settled.status, 201);\n\n    const { rows } = await pool.query<{ status: string; amount: number }>(`\n      select sp.status, s.amount\n      from settlement_periods sp\n      join settlements s on s.period_id = sp.id\n      where sp.year = $1 and sp.month = $2\n    `, [testPeriod.year, testPeriod.month]);\n    assert.deepEqual(rows, [{ status: "settled", amount: 5000 }]);\n  });\n});\n''')

    ('from "../../../lib/db"', 'from "../../../lib/db.ts"'),
  ],

(root / 'tests/e2e/kakeibo.spec.ts').write_text('''import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";\n\nimport { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";\n\nfunction resetTestDatabase() {\n  execFileSync("docker", [\n    "compose",\n    "exec",\n    "-T",\n    "db",\n    "/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/002_prepare_local_environments.sh",\n  ], { stdio: "ignore" });\n}\n\ntest.beforeEach(() => {\n  resetTestDatabase();\n});\n\ntest.afterAll(() => {\n  resetTestDatabase();\n});\n\ntest("月を切り替えると過去月では支出追加を無効化する", async ({ page }) => {\n  await page.goto("/");\n\n  await expect(page.getByText("2026年5月")).toBeVisible();\n  await expect(page.getByText("駅前イタリアン")).toBeVisible();\n\n  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "前月を見る" }).click();\n\n  await expect(page.getByText("2026年4月")).toBeVisible();\n  await expect(page.getByLabel("支出を入力")).toBeDisabled();\n  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "記録する" })).toBeDisabled();\n\n  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "翌月を見る" }).click();\n  await expect(page.getByText("2026年5月")).toBeVisible();\n  await expect(page.getByLabel("支出を入力")).toBeEnabled();\n});\n\ntest("要確認を共通費へ整理して月次精算を確定する", async ({ page }) => {\n  await page.goto("/");\n\n  const reviewCard = page.locator(".review-card").filter({ hasText: "駅前イタリアン" });\n  await expect(reviewCard).toBeVisible();\n  await reviewCard.getByRole("button", { name: "共通費", exact: true }).click();\n  await expect(reviewCard).toHaveCount(0);\n\n  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "精算", exact: true }).click();\n  const settleButton = page.getByRole("button", { name: "精算済みにする" });\n  await expect(settleButton).toBeEnabled();\n  await settleButton.click();\n\n  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "精算済み", exact: true })).toBeDisabled();\n});\n''')

from pathlib import Path
file = Path('/home/haya-ryoo/apps/kakeibo/src/app/api/expenses/[id]/route.ts')
text = file.read_text()

def replace(old, new):
    global text
    if old not in text:
        raise SystemExit(f'missing marker: {old[:100]!r}')
    text = text.replace(old, new, 1)

replace('''const updateExpenseBodySchema = z.object({\n  scope: z.enum(["shared", "personal", "review"]),\n  splitPreset: z.enum(["equal", "a70", "b70"]).optional().transform(val => val || "equal"),\n});\n''', '''const updateExpenseBodySchema = z.object({\n  scope: z.enum(["shared", "personal", "review"]),\n  splitPreset: z.enum(["equal", "a70", "b70"]).optional().transform(val => val || "equal"),\n  title: z.string().trim().min(1).max(100).optional(),\n  amount: z.number().int().positive().optional(),\n  paidBy: z.enum(["a", "b"]).optional(),\n  category: z.string().trim().min(1).max(50).optional(),\n  note: z.string().trim().max(200).optional(),\n  expenseDate: z.string().date().optional(),\n});\n''')
replace('''type HouseholdRow = { id: string };\ntype MemberRow = { id: string };\n''', '''type HouseholdRow = { id: string };\ntype MemberRow = { id: string };\ntype CategoryRow = { id: string };\ntype PaymentSourceRow = { id: string };\n''')
replace('''const expenseQuery = `\n  select id\n  from expenses\n  where id = $1 and household_id = $2\n`;\n\nconst updateExpenseQuery = `\n  update expenses\n  set scope = $2\n  where id = $1\n`;\n''', '''const expenseQuery = `\n  select id, memo\n  from expenses\n  where id = $1 and household_id = $2\n`;\n\nconst updateExpenseQuery = `\n  update expenses\n  set\n    paid_by_member_id = coalesce($2, paid_by_member_id),\n    payment_source_id = coalesce($3, payment_source_id),\n    category_id = coalesce($4, category_id),\n    amount = coalesce($5, amount),\n    expense_date = coalesce($6, expense_date),\n    scope = $7,\n    memo = coalesce($8, memo)\n  where id = $1\n`;\n\nconst deleteExpenseQuery = `\n  delete from expenses\n  where id = $1 and household_id = $2\n  returning id\n`;\n\nconst categoryQuery = `\n  select id\n  from categories\n  where household_id = $1 and name = $2\n  limit 1\n`;\n\nconst createCategoryQuery = `\n  insert into categories (household_id, name, kind, default_expense_scope, default_split_type)\n  values ($1, $2, 'other', $3, 'equal')\n  on conflict (household_id, name) do update\n  set name = excluded.name\n  returning id\n`;\n\nconst paymentSourceQuery = `\n  select id\n  from payment_sources\n  where household_id = $1 and owner_member_id = $2 and type = 'member_personal'\n  order by created_at asc\n  limit 1\n`;\n\nconst fallbackPaymentSourceQuery = `\n  select id\n  from payment_sources\n  where household_id = $1\n  order by type desc, created_at asc\n  limit 1\n`;\n''')
replace('''function splitRatesFor(preset: SplitPreset) {\n  if (preset === "a70") return [0.7, 0.3];\n  if (preset === "b70") return [0.3, 0.7];\n  return [0.5, 0.5];\n}\n''', '''function splitRatesFor(preset: SplitPreset) {\n  if (preset === "a70") return [0.7, 0.3];\n  if (preset === "b70") return [0.3, 0.7];\n  return [0.5, 0.5];\n}\n\nfunction memoFor(title: string, note: string) {\n  return note ? `${title} ${note}` : title;\n}\n''')
replace('''    const { scope, splitPreset } = result.data;\n''', '''    const { scope, splitPreset, title, amount, paidBy, category, note, expenseDate } = result.data;\n''')
replace('''      const expense = (await client.query(expenseQuery, [id, household.id])).rows[0];\n''', '''      const expense = (await client.query<{ id: string; memo: string | null }>(expenseQuery, [id, household.id])).rows[0];\n''')
replace('''      await client.query(updateExpenseQuery, [id, scope]);\n      await client.query(deleteSplitsQuery, [id]);\n\n      if (scope === "shared") {\n        const members = (await client.query<MemberRow>(membersQuery, [household.id])).rows;\n        const rates = splitRatesFor(splitPreset);\n''', '''      const members = (await client.query<MemberRow>(membersQuery, [household.id])).rows;\n      const payer = paidBy ? (paidBy === "a" ? members[0] : members[1]) : undefined;\n      if (paidBy && !payer) {\n        await client.query("rollback");\n        return NextResponse.json({ error: "payer_not_found" }, { status: 400 });\n      }\n\n      const categoryId = category\n        ? (await client.query<CategoryRow>(categoryQuery, [household.id, category])).rows[0]?.id\n          ?? (await client.query<CategoryRow>(createCategoryQuery, [household.id, category, scope])).rows[0]?.id\n        : undefined;\n      if (category && !categoryId) {\n        await client.query("rollback");\n        return NextResponse.json({ error: "category_not_found" }, { status: 400 });\n      }\n\n      const paymentSourceId = payer\n        ? (await client.query<PaymentSourceRow>(paymentSourceQuery, [household.id, payer.id])).rows[0]?.id\n          ?? (await client.query<PaymentSourceRow>(fallbackPaymentSourceQuery, [household.id])).rows[0]?.id\n        : undefined;\n      if (payer && !paymentSourceId) {\n        await client.query("rollback");\n        return NextResponse.json({ error: "payment_source_not_found" }, { status: 400 });\n      }\n\n      const memo = title !== undefined || note !== undefined\n        ? memoFor(title ?? expense.memo?.split(" ")[0] ?? "支出", note ?? "")\n        : undefined;\n\n      await client.query(updateExpenseQuery, [id, payer?.id, paymentSourceId, categoryId, amount, expenseDate, scope, memo]);\n      await client.query(deleteSplitsQuery, [id]);\n\n      if (scope === "shared") {\n        const rates = splitRatesFor(splitPreset);\n''')
text += '''\nexport async function DELETE(_request: Request, context: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {\n  try {\n    const { id } = await context.params;\n    if (!uuidSchema.safeParse(id).success) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_expense_id" }, { status: 400 });\n    }\n\n    const pool = db();\n    const household = (await pool.query<HouseholdRow>(householdQuery)).rows[0];\n    if (!household) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: "household_not_found" }, { status: 404 });\n    }\n\n    const deleted = (await pool.query(deleteExpenseQuery, [id, household.id])).rows[0];\n    if (!deleted) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: "expense_not_found" }, { status: 404 });\n    }\n\n    return NextResponse.json({ deletedExpenseId: id });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error("Failed to delete expense", error);\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: "expense_delete_failed" }, { status: 500 });\n  }\n}\n'''
file.write_text(text)

from pathlib import Path
file = Path('/home/haya-ryoo/apps/kakeibo/src/components/kakeibo-dashboard.tsx')
text = file.read_text()

def replace(old, new):
    global text
    if old not in text:
        raise SystemExit(f'missing marker: {old[:120]!r}')
    text = text.replace(old, new, 1)

replace('''import { Camera, Check, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Home, List, Plus, ReceiptText, Send, Settings, Wallet } from "lucide-react";\n''', '''import { Camera, Check, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Home, List, Pencil, Plus, ReceiptText, Save, Send, Settings, Trash2, Wallet, X } from "lucide-react";\n''')
replace('''type CreateExpenseResponse = {\n  expense: Expense;\n};\n''', '''type CreateExpenseResponse = {\n  expense: Expense;\n};\n\ntype ExpenseEditDraft = {\n  title: string;\n  amount: string;\n  paidBy: MemberId;\n  category: string;\n  note: string;\n  expenseDate: string;\n};\n''')
replace('''  const [pendingExpenseUpdates, setPendingExpenseUpdates] = useState(0);\n  const [isSettling, setIsSettling] = useState(false);\n''', '''  const [pendingExpenseUpdates, setPendingExpenseUpdates] = useState(0);\n  const [editingExpenseId, setEditingExpenseId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [expenseEditDraft, setExpenseEditDraft] = useState<ExpenseEditDraft | null>(null);\n  const [isSettling, setIsSettling] = useState(false);\n''')
marker = '''  const settlePeriod = async () => {\n'''
insert = '''  const startExpenseEdit = (expense: Expense) => {\n    setEditingExpenseId(expense.id);\n    setExpenseEditDraft({\n      title: expense.title,\n      amount: String(expense.amount),\n      paidBy: expense.paidBy,\n      category: expense.category,\n      note: expense.note ?? "",\n      expenseDate: expense.createdAt.slice(0, 10),\n    });\n  };\n\n  const cancelExpenseEdit = () => {\n    setEditingExpenseId(null);\n    setExpenseEditDraft(null);\n  };\n\n  const saveExpenseEdit = async (expense: Expense) => {\n    if (!expenseEditDraft) return;\n\n    const amount = Number(expenseEditDraft.amount);\n    if (!Number.isInteger(amount) || amount <= 0 || !expenseEditDraft.title.trim() || !expenseEditDraft.category.trim()) {\n      setSaveError("金額、タイトル、カテゴリを確認してください。");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const nextExpense: Expense = {\n      ...expense,\n      title: expenseEditDraft.title.trim(),\n      amount,\n      paidBy: expenseEditDraft.paidBy,\n      category: expenseEditDraft.category.trim(),\n      note: expenseEditDraft.note.trim() || undefined,\n      createdAt: `${expenseEditDraft.expenseDate}T00:00:00.000Z`,\n    };\n\n    setSaveError(null);\n    setPendingExpenseUpdates((current) => current + 1);\n\n    try {\n      const response = await fetch(`/api/expenses/${expense.id}`, {\n        method: "PATCH",\n        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n        body: JSON.stringify({\n          scope: expense.scope,\n          splitPreset: expense.splitPreset,\n          title: nextExpense.title,\n          amount: nextExpense.amount,\n          paidBy: nextExpense.paidBy,\n          category: nextExpense.category,\n          note: nextExpense.note ?? "",\n          expenseDate: expenseEditDraft.expenseDate,\n        }),\n      });\n\n      if (!response.ok) throw new Error("expense_edit_failed");\n      setExpenses((current) => current.map((item) => item.id === expense.id ? nextExpense : item));\n      cancelExpenseEdit();\n    } catch {\n      setSaveError("支出を更新できませんでした。入力内容を確認してください。");\n    } finally {\n      setPendingExpenseUpdates((current) => Math.max(0, current - 1));\n    }\n  };\n\n  const deleteExpense = async (expense: Expense) => {\n    if (!window.confirm(`${expense.title}を削除しますか？`)) return;\n\n    setSaveError(null);\n    setPendingExpenseUpdates((current) => current + 1);\n\n    try {\n      const response = await fetch(`/api/expenses/${expense.id}`, { method: "DELETE" });\n      if (!response.ok) throw new Error("expense_delete_failed");\n      setExpenses((current) => current.filter((item) => item.id !== expense.id));\n      if (editingExpenseId === expense.id) cancelExpenseEdit();\n    } catch {\n      setSaveError("支出を削除できませんでした。通信状態を確認してください。");\n    } finally {\n      setPendingExpenseUpdates((current) => Math.max(0, current - 1));\n    }\n  };\n\n'''
if marker not in text:
    raise SystemExit('settle marker missing')
text = text.replace(marker, insert + marker, 1)
replace('''  const expenseCard = (expense: Expense, options?: { showSplitPicker?: boolean }) => (\n''', '''  const expenseCard = (expense: Expense, options?: { showSplitPicker?: boolean; showManagement?: boolean }) => (\n''')
replace('''      <div className="timeline-item__actions">\n''', '''      {options?.showManagement ? (\n        <div className="timeline-item__manage">\n          <button className="chip" type="button" onClick={() => startExpenseEdit(expense)}>\n            <Pencil size={14} />\n            編集\n          </button>\n          <button className="chip chip--danger" type="button" onClick={() => void deleteExpense(expense)}>\n            <Trash2 size={14} />\n            削除\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      ) : null}\n\n      {editingExpenseId === expense.id && expenseEditDraft ? (\n        <form className="expense-editor" onSubmit={(event) => {\n          event.preventDefault();\n          void saveExpenseEdit(expense);\n        }}>\n          <label>\n            <span>タイトル</span>\n            <input value={expenseEditDraft.title} onChange={(event) => setExpenseEditDraft({ ...expenseEditDraft, title: event.target.value })} />\n          </label>\n          <label>\n            <span>金額</span>\n            <input inputMode="numeric" value={expenseEditDraft.amount} onChange={(event) => setExpenseEditDraft({ ...expenseEditDraft, amount: event.target.value })} />\n          </label>\n          <label>\n            <span>日付</span>\n            <input type="date" value={expenseEditDraft.expenseDate} onChange={(event) => setExpenseEditDraft({ ...expenseEditDraft, expenseDate: event.target.value })} />\n          </label>\n          <label>\n            <span>支払者</span>\n            <select value={expenseEditDraft.paidBy} onChange={(event) => setExpenseEditDraft({ ...expenseEditDraft, paidBy: event.target.value as MemberId })}>\n              {members.map((member) => <option key={member.id} value={member.id}>{member.name}</option>)}\n            </select>\n          </label>\n          <label>\n            <span>カテゴリ</span>\n            <input value={expenseEditDraft.category} onChange={(event) => setExpenseEditDraft({ ...expenseEditDraft, category: event.target.value })} />\n          </label>\n          <label className="expense-editor__wide">\n            <span>メモ</span>\n            <input value={expenseEditDraft.note} onChange={(event) => setExpenseEditDraft({ ...expenseEditDraft, note: event.target.value })} />\n          </label>\n          <div className="expense-editor__actions">\n            <button className="chip chip--active" type="submit">\n              <Save size={14} />\n              保存\n            </button>\n            <button className="chip" type="button" onClick={cancelExpenseEdit}>\n              <X size={14} />\n              キャンセル\n            </button>\n          </div>\n        </form>\n      ) : null}\n\n      <div className="timeline-item__actions">\n''')
replace('''      <div className="timeline timeline--wide">{timeline.map((expense) => expenseCard(expense))}</div>\n''', '''      <div className="timeline timeline--wide">{timeline.map((expense) => expenseCard(expense, { showManagement: true }))}</div>\n''')
file.write_text(text)

replace('''    await pool.query("delete from expenses where id = $1", [testExpenseId]);\n''', '''    await pool.query("delete from expenses where id = any($1::uuid[])", [[testExpenseId, editExpenseId, deleteExpenseId]]);\n''')

insert = '''  it("edits expense details and deletes an expense", async () => {\n    const { DELETE, PATCH } = await import("../../src/app/api/expenses/[id]/route.ts");\n    const editResponse = await PATCH(\n      new Request(`http://localhost/api/expenses/${editExpenseId}`, {\n        method: "PATCH",\n        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n        body: JSON.stringify({\n          scope: "personal",\n          title: "編集後",\n          amount: 3600,\n          paidBy: "b",\n          category: "交通費",\n          note: "電車代",\n          expenseDate: "2098-11-12",\n        }),\n      }),\n      { params: Promise.resolve({ id: editExpenseId }) },\n    );\n    assert.equal(editResponse.status, 200);\n\n    const { rows } = await pool.query<{ amount: number; expense_date: string; memo: string; category: string }>(`\n      select e.amount, e.expense_date::text, e.memo, c.name as category\n      from expenses e\n      join categories c on c.id = e.category_id\n      where e.id = $1\n    `, [editExpenseId]);\n    assert.deepEqual(rows, [{ amount: 3600, expense_date: "2098-11-12", memo: "編集後 電車代", category: "交通費" }]);\n\n    const deleteResponse = await DELETE(\n      new Request(`http://localhost/api/expenses/${deleteExpenseId}`, { method: "DELETE" }),\n      { params: Promise.resolve({ id: deleteExpenseId }) },\n    );\n    assert.equal(deleteResponse.status, 200);\n    assert.equal((await pool.query("select id from expenses where id = $1", [deleteExpenseId])).rowCount, 0);\n  });\n\n'''

insert = '''  it("rejects expense creation for a settled current period", async () => {
    const { POST } = await import("../../src/app/api/expenses/route.ts");
    const household = (await pool.query<{ id: string }>("select id::text from households order by created_at asc limit 1")).rows[0];
    assert.ok(household);

    await pool.query(`
      insert into settlement_periods (household_id, year, month, status, settled_at)
      values ($1, extract(year from current_date)::int, extract(month from current_date)::int, 'settled', now())
      on conflict (household_id, year, month) do update
      set status = 'settled', settled_at = now()
    `, [household.id]);

    try {
      const response = await POST(new Request("http://localhost/api/expenses", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          title: "精算後の支出",
          amount: 1200,
          paidBy: "a",
          scope: "shared",
          category: "食費",
        }),
      }));

      assert.equal(response.status, 409);
    } finally {
      await pool.query(`
        update settlement_periods
        set status = 'open', settled_at = null
        where household_id = $1
          and year = extract(year from current_date)::int
          and month = extract(month from current_date)::int
      `, [household.id]);
    }
  });

'''

gh issue create --title '負担割合を大まかなプリセットから選べるようにする' --body $'## 概要\n共通費の金額の分け方を、毎回細かく入力せず大まかな割合から選べるようにする。\n\n## 背景\n現在は均等または限定的な割合を扱っている。実運用では、厳密な金額指定よりも `5:5`、`4:6`、`6:4` などの大まかな負担割合を素早く選べる方が使いやすい。\n\n## 要件案\n- 共通費の登録・編集時に負担割合プリセットを選択できる\n- 少なくとも `5:5`、`4:6`、`6:4` を扱う\n- 月次精算の計算に選択した割合を反映する\n- 履歴と精算画面で適用中の割合を確認できる\n- 既存データは `5:5` として扱う\n\n## 完了条件\n- 支出ごとに割合を保存できる\n- 精算額が選択した割合に基づいて計算される\n- API・計算ロジック・主要導線のテストが追加されている\n\n## 補足\n将来的に任意割合入力を追加できる構造にするが、最初はプリセット選択を優先する。'
gh issue create --title 'レシート明細から支出を起票できるようにする' --body $'## 概要\nレシートを起点に、支出を明細単位で起票できるようにする。\n\n## 背景\n1枚のレシートに共通費と個人費が混在する場合、合計金額だけでは正確に分けられない。細かく管理したいケースでは、レシート明細から支出候補を作成し、必要な行だけ分類できるようにしたい。\n\n## 要件案\n- レシート画像を登録できる\n- OCR 結果から店舗名、日付、合計金額、明細行を支出候補として表示する\n- 明細行ごとに共通費・個人費・要確認を選択できる\n- 認識誤りを手動修正できる\n- 確定前に合計金額と明細合計の差分を確認できる\n- OCR に失敗した場合も手入力へ切り替えられる\n\n## 完了条件\n- レシート画像から複数の支出候補を作成できる\n- 明細単位の分類と修正ができる\n- OCR 失敗時のフォールバックがある\n- 主要導線のテストが追加されている\n\n## 検討事項\n- OCR サービスの選定\n- 画像保存先と保持期間\n- レシート画像に含まれる個人情報の扱い\n- MVP では画像アップロードと手動補正を優先し、自動分類は後続でもよい'
gh issue create --title 'ユーザー登録とカップル連携の仕組みを追加する' --body $'## 概要\nユーザー登録から、2人で同じ世帯データを利用できるカップル連携までの仕組みを追加する。\n\n## 背景\n現在の API は最古の世帯を暗黙的に選択する MVP 実装であり、ユーザーごとのデータ分離がない。実利用には認証と世帯単位のアクセス制御が必要。\n\n## 要件案\n- ユーザー登録、ログイン、ログアウトを提供する\n- 初回登録時に世帯を作成できる\n- 招待 URL または招待コードでパートナーを世帯へ追加できる\n- 1世帯に紐づくメンバーと権限を管理する\n- API はログイン中ユーザーの所属世帯だけを参照する\n- 他世帯の支出・精算データへアクセスできない\n- 招待の期限切れ、再送、無効化を扱う\n\n## 完了条件\n- 2ユーザーが同じ世帯へ参加できる\n- 世帯ごとに支出・精算データが分離される\n- 未認証アクセスと他世帯アクセスが拒否される\n- 認証、招待、世帯分離の統合テストが追加されている\n\n## 検討事項\n- Supabase Auth と独自認証の比較\n- 招待フローの UI\n- 世帯を離脱・解消する場合のデータ保持方針\n- RLS を利用する場合の API 構成'

gh issue create --title '少額支出の仮登録とまとめ整理を追加する' --body $'## ユーザーストーリー\nコンビニなどの少額支出は、その場で細かく分類すると面倒だが、後回しにすると忘れる。数秒で仮登録して後からまとめて整えたい。\n\n## 要件案\n- 金額と店舗名だけで仮登録できる\n- PWA のホーム画面ショートカットから入力画面を直接開ける\n- 未分類・要確認の支出を一覧でまとめて整理できる\n- まとめ整理画面で分類と割合を連続編集できる\n\n## 完了条件\n- 最小入力で支出を保存できる\n- 未整理件数がホーム画面で分かる\n- 複数件を画面遷移なしで整理できる'
gh issue create --title '定期支出の自動起票と引き落とし通知を追加する' --body $'## ユーザーストーリー\n家賃、光熱費、サブスクなど、毎月発生する支出を何度も手入力したくない。金額の変化と引き落とし日だけ確認したい。\n\n## 要件案\n- 定期支出の名称、カテゴリ、支払者、予定日、標準金額を登録する\n- 毎月の支出候補を自動生成する\n- 前月との差額を表示する\n- 引き落とし予定日前に通知する\n- 確定、スキップ、金額修正を行える\n\n## 完了条件\n- 毎月の候補が二重登録されない\n- 前月差分が確認できる\n- 通知の有効・無効を設定できる'
gh issue create --title '給料日と共通口座への入金管理を追加する' --body $'## ユーザーストーリー\n給料日に、今月の生活費として共通口座へいくら入れるか決めたい。収入差がある月も無理のない負担にしたい。\n\n## 要件案\n- メンバーごとの給料日と収入を登録する\n- 給料日前後に通知する\n- 収入比率に応じた共通生活費の負担額を提案する\n- 共通口座への入金予定と完了状態を管理する\n- 月ごとに提案額を手動調整できる\n\n## 完了条件\n- 収入比率から入金提案額が計算される\n- 二人の入金状況を一覧で確認できる\n- 収入額の公開範囲を検討し、仕様として明記する\n\n## 関連\n- #2 負担割合を大まかなプリセットから選べるようにする'

gh issue create --title '月次精算を取り消して再編集できるようにする' --body $'## ユーザーストーリー\n月末精算を確定した後に、登録漏れや分類ミスに気付いた。確定済みデータを黙って書き換えず、取り消し理由を残して修正したい。\n\n## 要件案\n- 精算済み月に `精算を取り消す` 操作を追加する\n- 取り消し理由と実行者、日時を保存する\n- 取り消し後は支出の追加・編集・削除を再び許可する\n- 再確定時に精算額を再計算する\n- 過去の確定額と取り消し履歴を確認できる\n\n## 完了条件\n- 明示的な取り消しなしで精算済み月を変更できない\n- 取り消しと再確定の履歴が残る\n- API と主要導線のテストが追加されている'

python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path

local_schema = Path('postgres/migrations/001_initial_schema.sql')
text = local_schema.read_text()
needle = '''create table settlements (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  period_id uuid not null references settlement_periods(id) on delete cascade,
  from_member_id uuid not null references household_members(id) on delete cascade,
  to_member_id uuid not null references household_members(id) on delete cascade,
  amount integer not null,
  status settlement_status not null default 'pending',
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  constraint settlements_amount_positive check (amount > 0),
  constraint settlements_members_different check (from_member_id <> to_member_id)
);
'''
insert = needle + '''
create table settlement_period_events (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  period_id uuid not null references settlement_periods(id) on delete cascade,
  action text not null,
  reason text,
  acted_by_member_id uuid references household_members(id) on delete set null,
  settlement_snapshot jsonb,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  constraint settlement_period_events_action_valid check (action in ('settled', 'reopened')),
  constraint settlement_period_events_reopen_reason_required check (action <> 'reopened' or nullif(trim(reason), '') is not null)
);
'''
if needle not in text:
    raise SystemExit('local schema table insertion point missing')
text = text.replace(needle, insert, 1)
text = text.replace('''create index settlements_period_id_idx on settlements(period_id);
''', '''create index settlements_period_id_idx on settlements(period_id);
create index settlement_period_events_period_id_idx on settlement_period_events(period_id, created_at desc);
''', 1)
local_schema.write_text(text)

local_migration = Path('postgres/migrations/004_add_settlement_period_events.sql')
local_migration.write_text('''create table if not exists settlement_period_events (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  period_id uuid not null references settlement_periods(id) on delete cascade,
  action text not null,
  reason text,
  acted_by_member_id uuid references household_members(id) on delete set null,
  settlement_snapshot jsonb,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  constraint settlement_period_events_action_valid check (action in ('settled', 'reopened')),
  constraint settlement_period_events_reopen_reason_required check (action <> 'reopened' or nullif(trim(reason), '') is not null)
);

create index if not exists settlement_period_events_period_id_idx
  on settlement_period_events(period_id, created_at desc);
''')

prep = Path('postgres/migrations/002_prepare_local_environments.sh')
text = prep.read_text()

supabase = Path('supabase/migrations/202606010001_add_settlement_period_events.sql')
supabase.write_text('''create table public.settlement_period_events (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  period_id uuid not null references public.settlement_periods(id) on delete cascade,
  action text not null,
  reason text,
  acted_by_member_id uuid references public.household_members(id) on delete set null,
  settlement_snapshot jsonb,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  constraint settlement_period_events_action_valid check (action in ('settled', 'reopened')),
  constraint settlement_period_events_reopen_reason_required check (action <> 'reopened' or nullif(trim(reason), '') is not null)
);

create index settlement_period_events_period_id_idx
  on public.settlement_period_events(period_id, created_at desc);

alter table public.settlement_period_events enable row level security;

create policy "members can manage settlement period events"
on public.settlement_period_events
for all
using (
  exists (
    select 1
    from public.settlement_periods sp
    where sp.id = period_id
      and public.is_household_member(sp.household_id)
  )
)
with check (
  exists (
    select 1
    from public.settlement_periods sp
    where sp.id = period_id
      and public.is_household_member(sp.household_id)
  )
);
''')

reopen = Path('src/app/api/settlements/reopen/route.ts')
reopen.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
reopen.write_text('''import { NextResponse } from "next/server.js";
import { z } from "zod";
import { db } from "../../../../lib/db.ts";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const reopenSettlementSchema = z.object({
  year: z.number().int().min(2000).max(9999),
  month: z.number().int().min(1).max(12),
  reason: z.string().trim().min(1).max(200),
});

type HouseholdRow = { id: string };
type SettlementPeriodRow = { id: string };

// TODO(security): 認証統合時に、ログイン中ユーザーの所属世帯と acted_by_member_id をセッションから解決する。
const householdQuery = `
  select id
  from households
  order by created_at asc
  limit 1
`;

const settledPeriodQuery = `
  select id::text
  from settlement_periods
  where household_id = $1 and year = $2 and month = $3 and status = 'settled'
  for update
`;

const settlementSnapshotQuery = `
  select coalesce(json_agg(json_build_object(
    'fromMemberId', from_member_id::text,
    'toMemberId', to_member_id::text,
    'amount', amount,
    'status', status
  ) order by created_at asc), '[]'::json) as transfers
  from settlements
  where period_id = $1
`;

const reopenPeriodQuery = `
  update settlement_periods
  set status = 'open', settled_at = null
  where id = $1
`;

const deleteSettlementsQuery = `
  delete from settlements
  where period_id = $1
`;

const insertSettlementEventQuery = `
  insert into settlement_period_events (period_id, action, reason, settlement_snapshot)
  values ($1, 'reopened', $2, $3::jsonb)
`;

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  let json: unknown;
  try {
    json = await request.json();
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_reopen_request" }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const result = reopenSettlementSchema.safeParse(json);
  if (!result.success) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_reopen_request" }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const { year, month, reason } = result.data;
  const pool = db();
  const client = await pool.connect();

  try {
    await client.query("begin");

    const household = (await client.query<HouseholdRow>(householdQuery)).rows[0];
    if (!household) {
      await client.query("rollback");
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "household_not_found" }, { status: 404 });
    }

    const period = (await client.query<SettlementPeriodRow>(settledPeriodQuery, [household.id, year, month])).rows[0];
    if (!period) {
      await client.query("rollback");
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "settled_period_not_found" }, { status: 404 });
    }

    const snapshot = (await client.query<{ transfers: unknown }>(settlementSnapshotQuery, [period.id])).rows[0]?.transfers ?? [];
    await client.query(insertSettlementEventQuery, [period.id, reason, JSON.stringify({ transfers: snapshot })]);
    await client.query(deleteSettlementsQuery, [period.id]);
    await client.query(reopenPeriodQuery, [period.id]);
    await client.query("commit");

    return NextResponse.json({ period: { year, month, status: "open" } });
  } catch (error) {
    await client.query("rollback");
    console.error("Failed to reopen settlement period", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "settlement_reopen_failed" }, { status: 500 });
  } finally {
    client.release();
  }
}
''')
PY

from pathlib import Path

route = Path('src/app/api/expenses/suggestions/route.ts')
route.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
route.write_text('''import { NextResponse } from "next/server.js";
import { z } from "zod";
import { db } from "../../../../lib/db.ts";
import type { ExpenseScope } from "../../../../lib/expense-utils.ts";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const suggestionsQuerySchema = z.object({
  q: z.string().trim().max(100).optional().transform((value) => value ?? ""),
});

type HouseholdRow = { id: string };
type SuggestionRow = {
  title: string;
  category: string;
  scope: ExpenseScope;
  latest_amount: number;
  usage_count: number;
};

// TODO(security): 認証統合時に、ログイン中ユーザーの所属世帯をセッションから解決する。
const householdQuery = `
  select id
  from households
  order by created_at asc
  limit 1
`;

const suggestionsQuery = `
  with expense_history as (
    select
      coalesce(nullif(split_part(e.memo, ' ', 1), ''), c.name) as title,
      c.name as category,
      e.scope,
      e.amount,
      e.created_at,
      count(*) over (
        partition by coalesce(nullif(split_part(e.memo, ' ', 1), ''), c.name), c.name, e.scope
      ) as variant_count
    from expenses e
    join categories c on c.id = e.category_id
    where e.household_id = $1
      and ($2 = '' or coalesce(nullif(split_part(e.memo, ' ', 1), ''), c.name) ilike '%' || $2 || '%')
  ),
  ranked_history as (
    select
      title,
      category,
      scope,
      amount as latest_amount,
      count(*) over (partition by title) as usage_count,
      row_number() over (
        partition by title
        order by variant_count desc, created_at desc
      ) as preference_rank
    from expense_history
  )
  select title, category, scope, latest_amount, usage_count::int
  from ranked_history
  where preference_rank = 1
  order by usage_count desc, title asc
  limit 6
`;

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  try {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    const result = suggestionsQuerySchema.safeParse({ q: url.searchParams.get("q") ?? undefined });
    if (!result.success) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_suggestion_query" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const pool = db();
    const household = (await pool.query<HouseholdRow>(householdQuery)).rows[0];
    if (!household) {
      return NextResponse.json({ suggestions: [] });
    }

    const suggestions = (await pool.query<SuggestionRow>(suggestionsQuery, [household.id, result.data.q])).rows;
    return NextResponse.json({
      suggestions: suggestions.map((suggestion) => ({
        title: suggestion.title,
        category: suggestion.category,
        scope: suggestion.scope,
        latestAmount: suggestion.latest_amount,
        usageCount: suggestion.usage_count,
      })),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Failed to load expense suggestions", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "expense_suggestions_failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
''')

    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = window.setTimeout(async () => {
      try {
        const response = await fetch(`/api/expenses/suggestions?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}`, { signal: controller.signal });
        if (!response.ok) return;
        const data = (await response.json()) as { suggestions: ExpenseSuggestion[] };
        setComposerSuggestions(data.suggestions);
      } catch (error) {
        if ((error as Error).name !== "AbortError") setComposerSuggestions([]);
      }
    }, 150);

    return () => {
      window.clearTimeout(timer);
      controller.abort();
    };
  }, [composerValue, isComposerExpanded, isCurrentPeriod, isPeriodLocked]);

'''

.composer__suggestions::-webkit-scrollbar {
  display: none;
}

.composer-suggestion {
  display: grid;
  min-width: max-content;
  gap: 2px;
  padding: 8px 10px;
  border: 1px solid rgba(15, 118, 110, 0.18);
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: var(--primary-ghost);
  color: var(--text);
  text-align: left;
}

.composer-suggestion span {
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 700;
}

.composer-suggestion small {
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 11px;
}

.composer:not([data-expanded="true"]) .composer__suggestions {
  display: none;
}

'''
if needle not in text:
    raise SystemExit('CSS suggestion insertion point missing')
css.write_text(text.replace(needle, insert + needle, 1))

from pathlib import Path
path = Path('src/app/api/expenses/suggestions/route.ts')
text = path.read_text()
old = '''const suggestionsQuery = `
  with expense_history as (
    select
      coalesce(nullif(split_part(e.memo, ' ', 1), ''), c.name) as title,
      c.name as category,
      e.scope,
      e.amount,
      e.created_at,
      count(*) over (
        partition by coalesce(nullif(split_part(e.memo, ' ', 1), ''), c.name), c.name, e.scope
      ) as variant_count
    from expenses e
    join categories c on c.id = e.category_id
    where e.household_id = $1
      and ($2 = '' or coalesce(nullif(split_part(e.memo, ' ', 1), ''), c.name) ilike '%' || $2 || '%')
  ),
'''
new = '''const suggestionsQuery = `
  with expense_sources as (
    select
      case
        when c.kind = 'rent' then c.name
        else coalesce(nullif(split_part(e.memo, ' ', 1), ''), c.name)
      end as title,
      c.name as category,
      e.scope,
      e.amount,
      e.created_at
    from expenses e
    join categories c on c.id = e.category_id
    where e.household_id = $1
  ),
  expense_history as (
    select
      title,
      category,
      scope,
      amount,
      created_at,
      count(*) over (
        partition by title, category, scope
      ) as variant_count
    from expense_sources
    where $2 = '' or title ilike '%' || $2 || '%'
  ),
'''
if old not in text:
    raise SystemExit('expected SQL block not found')
path.write_text(text.replace(old, new))
PY

from pathlib import Path

Path('src/app/api/analytics/route.ts').write_text('''import { NextResponse } from "next/server.js";\nimport { z } from "zod";\nimport { db } from "../../../lib/db.ts";\n\nexport const runtime = "nodejs";\nexport const dynamic = "force-dynamic";\n\ntype MonthlyRow = { period: string; month: number; total: number };\ntype CategoryRow = { category: string; total: number };\ntype MemberRow = { id: string };\n\nconst analyticsQuerySchema = z.object({\n  year: z.string().transform(Number).pipe(z.number().int().min(2000).max(9999)),\n  month: z.string().transform(Number).pipe(z.number().int().min(1).max(12)),\n  scope: z.enum(["all", "shared", "personal", "review"]).optional().default("all"),\n  paidBy: z.enum(["all", "a", "b"]).optional().default("all"),\n  category: z.string().trim().max(50).optional().default(""),\n});\n\n// TODO(security): 認証統合時に、ログイン中ユーザーの所属世帯をセッションから解決する。\nconst householdQuery = `\n  select id\n  from households\n  order by created_at asc\n  limit 1\n`;\n\nconst membersQuery = `\n  select id\n  from household_members\n  where household_id = $1\n  order by role asc, created_at asc\n`;\n\nconst monthlyQuery = `\n  with months as (\n    select generate_series(\n      make_date($2, $3, 1) - interval '5 months',\n      make_date($2, $3, 1),\n      interval '1 month'\n    )::date as month_start\n  )\n  select\n    to_char(months.month_start, 'YYYY-MM') as period,\n    extract(month from months.month_start)::int as month,\n    coalesce(sum(e.amount), 0)::int as total\n  from months\n  left join expenses e\n    on e.household_id = $1\n    and e.expense_date >= months.month_start\n    and e.expense_date < months.month_start + interval '1 month'\n    and ($4 = 'all' or e.scope = $4)\n    and ($5::uuid is null or e.paid_by_member_id = $5::uuid)\n    and ($6 = '' or exists (\n      select 1\n      from categories filtered_category\n      where filtered_category.id = e.category_id and filtered_category.name = $6\n    ))\n  group by months.month_start\n  order by months.month_start asc\n`;\n\nconst categoriesQuery = `\n  select c.name as category, sum(e.amount)::int as total\n  from expenses e\n  join categories c on c.id = e.category_id\n  where e.household_id = $1\n    and e.expense_date >= make_date($2, $3, 1)\n    and e.expense_date < make_date($2, $3, 1) + interval '1 month'\n    and ($4 = 'all' or e.scope = $4)\n    and ($5::uuid is null or e.paid_by_member_id = $5::uuid)\n    and ($6 = '' or c.name = $6)\n  group by c.name\n  order by total desc, c.name asc\n`;\n\nconst categoryOptionsQuery = `\n  select distinct name\n  from categories\n  where household_id = $1\n  order by name asc\n`;\n\nexport async function GET(request: Request) {\n  try {\n    const url = new URL(request.url);\n    const result = analyticsQuerySchema.safeParse({\n      year: url.searchParams.get("year") ?? "",\n      month: url.searchParams.get("month") ?? "",\n      scope: url.searchParams.get("scope") ?? undefined,\n      paidBy: url.searchParams.get("paidBy") ?? undefined,\n      category: url.searchParams.get("category") ?? undefined,\n    });\n\n    if (!result.success) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_analytics_query" }, { status: 400 });\n    }\n\n    const pool = db();\n    const household = (await pool.query<{ id: string }>(householdQuery)).rows[0];\n    if (!household) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ monthly: [], categories: [], categoryOptions: [] });\n    }\n\n    const { year, month, scope, paidBy, category } = result.data;\n    const members = (await pool.query<MemberRow>(membersQuery, [household.id])).rows;\n    const paidByMemberId = paidBy === "a" ? members[0]?.id ?? null : paidBy === "b" ? members[1]?.id ?? null : null;\n    const params = [household.id, year, month, scope, paidByMemberId, category];\n    const [monthly, categories, categoryOptions] = await Promise.all([\n      pool.query<MonthlyRow>(monthlyQuery, params),\n      pool.query<CategoryRow>(categoriesQuery, params),\n      pool.query<{ name: string }>(categoryOptionsQuery, [household.id]),\n    ]);\n\n    return NextResponse.json({\n      monthly: monthly.rows.map((item) => ({ ...item, label: `${item.month}月` })),\n      categories: categories.rows,\n      categoryOptions: categoryOptions.rows.map((item) => item.name),\n    });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error("Failed to load expense analytics", error);\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: "expense_analytics_failed" }, { status: 500 });\n  }\n}\n''')

path = Path('src/components/kakeibo-dashboard.tsx')
text = path.read_text()
anchor = '''type ExpenseEditDraft = {\n  title: string;\n  amount: string;\n  paidBy: MemberId;\n  category: string;\n  note: string;\n  expenseDate: string;\n};\n'''
addition = '''\ntype AnalyticsScope = "all" | ExpenseScope;\ntype AnalyticsPayer = "all" | MemberId;\ntype AnalyticsResponse = {\n  monthly: { period: string; label: string; total: number }[];\n  categories: { category: string; total: number }[];\n  categoryOptions: string[];\n};\n'''
if anchor not in text:
    raise SystemExit('type anchor not found')
text = text.replace(anchor, anchor + addition)
anchor = '''  const [isReopening, setIsReopening] = useState(false);\n  const composerInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);\n'''
addition = '''  const [analyticsScope, setAnalyticsScope] = useState<AnalyticsScope>("all");\n  const [analyticsPayer, setAnalyticsPayer] = useState<AnalyticsPayer>("all");\n  const [analyticsCategory, setAnalyticsCategory] = useState("");\n  const [analytics, setAnalytics] = useState<AnalyticsResponse | null>(null);\n  const [isAnalyticsLoading, setIsAnalyticsLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [analyticsError, setAnalyticsError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n'''
if anchor not in text:
    raise SystemExit('state anchor not found')
text = text.replace(anchor, '  const [isReopening, setIsReopening] = useState(false);\n' + addition + '  const composerInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);\n')
anchor = '''  useEffect(() => {\n    if (didHandleQuickCompose.current || isComposerDisabled) return;\n'''
addition = '''  useEffect(() => {\n    let active = true;\n    const params = new URLSearchParams({\n      year: String(selectedPeriod.year),\n      month: String(selectedPeriod.month),\n      scope: analyticsScope,\n      paidBy: analyticsPayer,\n    });\n    if (analyticsCategory) params.set("category", analyticsCategory);\n\n    setIsAnalyticsLoading(true);\n    setAnalyticsError(null);\n    void fetch(`/api/analytics?${params.toString()}`)\n      .then((response) => {\n        if (!response.ok) throw new Error("analytics_load_failed");\n        return response.json() as Promise<AnalyticsResponse>;\n      })\n      .then((data) => {\n        if (active) setAnalytics(data);\n      })\n      .catch(() => {\n        if (active) {\n          setAnalytics(null);\n          setAnalyticsError("使用料グラフを読み込めませんでした。");\n        }\n      })\n      .finally(() => {\n        if (active) setIsAnalyticsLoading(false);\n      });\n\n    return () => {\n      active = false;\n    };\n  }, [selectedPeriod, analyticsScope, analyticsPayer, analyticsCategory, expenses]);\n\n'''
if anchor not in text:
    raise SystemExit('effect anchor not found')
text = text.replace(anchor, addition + anchor)
anchor = '''  const homeView = (\n    <div className="layout-grid">\n      <section>\n'''
addition = '''  const monthlyMax = Math.max(...(analytics?.monthly.map((item) => item.total) ?? [0]), 1);\n  const categoryTotal = analytics?.categories.reduce((total, item) => total + item.total, 0) ?? 0;\n  const analyticsPanel = (\n    <section className="section analytics-panel" aria-label="使用料グラフ">\n      <div className="section__header">\n        <div>\n          <h2 className="section__title">使用料グラフ</h2>\n          <p className="section__meta">月別推移とカテゴリ内訳をざっくり確認する</p>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n      <div className="analytics-filters">\n        <select aria-label="分類で絞り込む" value={analyticsScope} onChange={(event) => setAnalyticsScope(event.target.value as AnalyticsScope)}>\n          <option value="all">すべての分類</option>\n          <option value="shared">共通費のみ</option>\n          <option value="personal">個人費のみ</option>\n          <option value="review">要確認のみ</option>\n        </select>\n        <select aria-label="支払者で絞り込む" value={analyticsPayer} onChange={(event) => setAnalyticsPayer(event.target.value as AnalyticsPayer)}>\n          <option value="all">すべての支払者</option>\n          {members.map((member) => <option key={member.id} value={member.id}>{member.name}</option>)}\n        </select>\n        <select aria-label="カテゴリで絞り込む" value={analyticsCategory} onChange={(event) => setAnalyticsCategory(event.target.value)}>\n          <option value="">すべてのカテゴリ</option>\n          {(analytics?.categoryOptions ?? []).map((category) => <option key={category} value={category}>{category}</option>)}\n        </select>\n      </div>\n      {isAnalyticsLoading ? <p className="analytics-empty">読み込み中...</p> : analyticsError ? <p className="analytics-empty">{analyticsError}</p> : (analytics?.categories.length ?? 0) === 0 ? <p className="analytics-empty">この条件の支出はまだありません。</p> : (\n        <div className="analytics-grid">\n          <article className="analytics-card">\n            <h3>直近6か月</h3>\n            <div className="monthly-chart">\n              {analytics?.monthly.map((item) => (\n                <div key={item.period} className="monthly-chart__item" aria-label={`${item.period}の支出 ${formatMoney(item.total)}`}>\n                  <strong>{formatMoney(item.total)}</strong>\n                  <div className="monthly-chart__track"><span style={{ height: `${Math.max(item.total / monthlyMax * 100, item.total > 0 ? 4 : 0)}%` }} /></div>\n                  <small>{item.label}</small>\n                </div>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n          </article>\n          <article className="analytics-card">\n            <h3>カテゴリ内訳</h3>\n            <div className="category-chart">\n              {analytics?.categories.map((item) => (\n                <div key={item.category} className="category-chart__item">\n                  <div><span>{item.category}</span><strong>{formatMoney(item.total)}</strong></div>\n                  <div className="category-chart__track"><span style={{ width: `${categoryTotal ? item.total / categoryTotal * 100 : 0}%` }} /></div>\n                </div>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n          </article>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </section>\n  );\n\n'''
if anchor not in text:
    raise SystemExit('home anchor not found')

var(--shadow);\n}\n\n.analytics-filters,\n.analytics-grid {\n  display: grid;\n  gap: 8px;\n}\n\n.analytics-filters select {\n  min-height: 40px;\n  padding: 0 10px;\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: 8px;\n  background: #fff;\n  color: var(--text);\n}\n\n.analytics-grid {\n  margin-top: 14px;\n}\n\n.analytics-card {\n  min-width: 0;\n  padding: 12px;\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: var(--radius);\n  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.64);\n}\n\n.analytics-card h3 {\n  margin: 0 0 12px;\n  color: var(--muted);\n  font-size: 13px;\n}\n\n.monthly-chart {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(6, minmax(38px, 1fr));\n  gap: 8px;\n  overflow-x: auto;\n}\n\n.monthly-chart__item {\n  display: grid;\n  min-width: 38px;\n  gap: 6px;\n  text-align: center;\n}\n\n.monthly-chart__item strong {\n  overflow: hidden;\n  color: var(--muted);\n  font-size: 10px;\n  text-overflow: ellipsis;\n}\n\n.monthly-chart__item small {\n  color: var(--muted);\n  font-size: 11px;\n}\n\n.monthly-chart__track {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: flex-end;\n  height: 116px;\n  border-radius: 6px;\n  background: rgba(15, 118, 110, 0.08);\n  overflow: hidden;\n}\n\n.monthly-chart__track span {\n  width: 100%;\n  border-radius: 6px 6px 0 0;\n  background: var(--primary);\n}\n\n.category-chart {\n  display: grid;\n  gap: inherit;\n  background: var(--primary);\n}\n\n.analytics-empty {\n  margin: 14px 0 0;\n  color: var(--muted);\n  font-size: 13px;\n}\n\n@media (min-width: 720px) {\n  .analytics-filters {\n    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));\n  }\n\n  .analytics-grid {\n    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1.1fr) minmax(0, 0.9fr);\n  }\n}\n''')
