from pathlib import Path

migration = '''create table if not exists recurring_expense_occurrences (\n  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),\n  recurring_expense_id uuid not null references recurring_expenses(id) on delete cascade,\n  year integer not null,\n  month integer not null,\n  status text not null,\n  expense_id uuid references expenses(id) on delete set null,\n  amount integer,\n  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),\n  updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),\n  unique (recurring_expense_id, year, month),\n  constraint recurring_expense_occurrences_month_range check (month between 1 and 12),\n  constraint recurring_expense_occurrences_status_valid check (status in ('confirmed', 'skipped')),\n  constraint recurring_expense_occurrences_amount_positive check (amount is null or amount > 0)\n);\n\ncreate index if not exists recurring_expense_occurrences_recurring_id_idx\n  on recurring_expense_occurrences(recurring_expense_id, year, month);\n'''
Path('postgres/migrations/005_add_recurring_expense_occurrences.sql').write_text(migration)
Path('supabase/migrations/202606020001_add_recurring_expense_occurrences.sql').write_text(migration.replace('recurring_expense_occurrences', 'public.recurring_expense_occurrences').replace('references recurring_expenses', 'references public.recurring_expenses').replace('references expenses', 'references public.expenses') + '''\nalter table public.recurring_expense_occurrences enable row level security;\n\ncreate policy "members can manage recurring expense occurrences"\non public.recurring_expense_occurrences\nfor all\nusing (\n  exists (\n    select 1 from public.recurring_expenses re\n    where re.id = recurring_expense_id\n      and public.is_household_member(re.household_id)\n  )\n)\nwith check (\n  exists (\n    select 1 from public.recurring_expenses re\n    where re.id = recurring_expense_id\n      and public.is_household_member(re.household_id)\n  )\n);\n''')

prepare = Path('postgres/migrations/002_prepare_local_environments.sh')
text = prepare.read_text()
text = text.replace('settlement_events_migration_file="/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/004_add_settlement_period_events.sql"', 'settlement_events_migration_file="/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/004_add_settlement_period_events.sql"\nrecurring_occurrences_migration_file="/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/005_add_recurring_expense_occurrences.sql"')
text = text.replace('  psql -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 --username "$POSTGRES_USER" --dbname "$database_name" \\\n    -f "$settlement_events_migration_file"', '  psql -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 --username "$POSTGRES_USER" --dbname "$database_name" \\\n    -f "$settlement_events_migration_file"\n  psql -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 --username "$POSTGRES_USER" --dbname "$database_name" \\\n    -f "$recurring_occurrences_migration_file"')
prepare.write_text(text)

Path('src/app/api/recurring-expenses/candidates/route.ts').write_text('''import { NextResponse } from "next/server.js";\nimport { z } from "zod";\nimport { db } from "../../../../lib/db.ts";\n\nexport const runtime = "nodejs";\nexport const dynamic = "force-dynamic";\n\nconst periodSchema = z.object({\n  year: z.number().int().min(2000).max(9999),\n  month: z.number().int().min(1).max(12),\n});\nconst querySchema = z.object({\n  year: z.string().transform(Number).pipe(z.number().int().min(2000).max(9999)),\n  month: z.string().transform(Number).pipe(z.number().int().min(1).max(12)),\n});\nconst actionSchema = periodSchema.extend({\n  recurringExpenseId: z.string().uuid(),\n  action: z.enum(["confirm", "skip"]),\n  amount: z.number().int().positive().optional(),\n});\n\nconst householdQuery = `select id from households order by created_at asc limit 1`;\nconst candidatesQuery = `\n  select re.id::text, c.name as title, c.name as category, re.amount, re.day_of_month, re.scope::text,\n    coalesce(reo.status, 'pending') as status, reo.expense_id::text,\n    greatest(make_date($2, $3, 1), least(\n      (make_date($2, $3, 1) + interval '1 month - 1 day')::date,\n      make_date($2, $3, 1) + (re.day_of_month - 1)\n    ))::text as due_date\n  from recurring_expenses re\n  join categories c on c.id = re.category_id\n  left join recurring_expense_occurrences reo\n    on reo.recurring_expense_id = re.id and reo.year = $2 and reo.month = $3\n  where re.household_id = $1 and re.active\n  order by re.day_of_month asc, c.name asc\n`;\nconst recurringQuery = `\n  select re.id, re.household_id, re.category_id, re.payment_source_id, re.paid_by_member_id,\n    re.amount, re.day_of_month, re.scope::text, re.split_type::text, c.name as title\n  from recurring_expenses re join categories c on c.id = re.category_id\n  where re.id = $1 and re.household_id = $2 and re.active\n  for update\n`;\nconst periodLockedQuery = `select exists (select 1 from settlement_periods where household_id = $1 and year = $2 and month = $3 and status = 'settled') as locked`;\nconst insertExpenseQuery = `\n  insert into expenses (household_id, paid_by_member_id, payment_source_id, category_id, amount, expense_date, scope, memo)\n  values ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, least(make_date($6, $7, $8), (make_date($6, $7, 1) + interval '1 month - 1 day')::date), $9, $10)\n  returning id\n`;\nconst insertOccurrenceQuery = `\n  insert into recurring_expense_occurrences (recurring_expense_id, year, month, status, expense_id, amount)\n  values ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6)\n  on conflict (recurring_expense_id, year, month) do nothing\n  returning id\n`;\nconst membersQuery = `select id, default_share_rate::float8 from household_members where household_id = $1 order by role asc, created_at asc`;\nconst insertSplitQuery = `insert into expense_splits (expense_id, member_id, rate) values ($1, $2, $3)`;\n\ntype Household = { id: string };\ntype Recurring = { id: string; household_id: string; category_id: string; payment_source_id: string; paid_by_member_id: string | null; amount: number; day_of_month: number; scope: string; split_type: string; title: string };\n\nfunction ratesFor(splitType: string, members: { id: string; default_share_rate: number | null }[]) {\n  if (splitType === "income_ratio" && members.every((member) => member.default_share_rate != null)) {\n    return members.map((member) => ({ id: member.id, rate: member.default_share_rate as number }));\n  }\n  return members.map((member) => ({ id: member.id, rate: 1 / members.length }));\n}\n\nexport async function GET(request: Request) {\n  try {\n    const url = new URL(request.url);\n    const result = querySchema.safeParse({ year: url.searchParams.get("year") ?? "", month: url.searchParams.get("month") ?? "" });\n    if (!result.success) return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_period" }, { status: 400 });\n    const pool = db();\n    const household = (await pool.query<Household>(householdQuery)).rows[0];\n    if (!household) return NextResponse.json({ candidates: [] });\n    const candidates = (await pool.query(candidatesQuery, [household.id, result.data.year, result.data.month])).rows;\n    return NextResponse.json({ candidates });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error("Failed to load recurring expense candidates", error);\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: "recurring_candidates_failed" }, { status: 500 });\n  }\n}\n\nexport async function POST(request: Request) {\n  const pool = db();\n  const client = await pool.connect();\n  try {\n    const result = actionSchema.safeParse(await request.json());\n    if (!result.success) return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_recurring_action" }, { status: 400 });\n    const { recurringExpenseId, year, month, action, amount } = result.data;\n    await client.query("begin");\n    const household = (await client.query<Household>(householdQuery)).rows[0];\n    if (!household) { await client.query("rollback"); return NextResponse.json({ error: "household_not_found" }, { status: 404 }); }\n    const locked = (await client.query<{ locked: boolean }>(periodLockedQuery, [household.id, year, month])).rows[0]?.locked;\n    if (locked) { await client.query("rollback"); return NextResponse.json({ error: "settled_period_locked" }, { status: 409 }); }\n    const recurring = (await client.query<Recurring>(recurringQuery, [recurringExpenseId, household.id])).rows[0];\n    if (!recurring) { await client.query("rollback"); return NextResponse.json({ error: "recurring_expense_not_found" }, { status: 404 }); }\n    const candidateAmount = amount ?? recurring.amount;\n    let expenseId: string | null = null;\n    if (action === "confirm") {\n      expenseId = (await client.query<{ id: string }>(insertExpenseQuery, [recurring.household_id, recurring.paid_by_member_id, recurring.payment_source_id, recurring.category_id, candidateAmount, year, month, recurring.day_of_month, recurring.scope, `${recurring.title} 定期支出`])).rows[0]?.id ?? null;\n      if (expenseId && recurring.scope === "shared") {\n        const members = (await client.query<{ id: string; default_share_rate: number | null }>(membersQuery, [household.id])).rows;\n        for (const member of ratesFor(recurring.split_type, members)) await client.query(insertSplitQuery, [expenseId, member.id, member.rate]);\n      }\n    }\n    const occurrence = (await client.query(insertOccurrenceQuery, [recurring.id, year, month, action === "confirm" ? "confirmed" : "skipped", expenseId, candidateAmount])).rows[0];\n    if (!occurrence) { await client.query("rollback"); return NextResponse.json({ error: "recurring_expense_already_handled" }, { status: 409 }); }\n    await client.query("commit");\n    console.info("Handled recurring expense candidate", { recurringExpenseId, year, month, action });\n    return NextResponse.json({ status: action === "confirm" ? "confirmed" : "skipped", expenseId }, { status: 201 });\n  } catch (error) {\n    await client.query("rollback");\n    console.error("Failed to handle recurring expense candidate", error);\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: "recurring_candidate_action_failed" }, { status: 500 });\n  } finally { client.release(); }\n}\n''')
PY

addition = '''  useEffect(() => {\n    let active = true;\n    setIsRecurringLoading(true);\n    setRecurringError(null);\n    void fetch(`/api/recurring-expenses/candidates?year=${selectedPeriod.year}&month=${selectedPeriod.month}`)\n      .then((response) => {\n        if (!response.ok) throw new Error("recurring_candidates_load_failed");\n        return response.json() as Promise<{ candidates: RecurringCandidate[] }>;\n      })\n      .then((data) => {\n        if (active) setRecurringCandidates(data.candidates);\n      })\n      .catch(() => {\n        if (active) {\n          setRecurringCandidates([]);\n          setRecurringError("定期支出を読み込めませんでした。");\n        }\n      })\n      .finally(() => {\n        if (active) setIsRecurringLoading(false);\n      });\n    return () => { active = false; };\n  }, [selectedPeriod, recurringRefresh]);\n\n  const handleRecurringCandidate = async (candidate: RecurringCandidate, action: "confirm" | "skip") => {\n    if (pendingRecurringId || candidate.status !== "pending") return;\n    setPendingRecurringId(candidate.id);\n    setRecurringError(null);\n    try {\n      const response = await fetch("/api/recurring-expenses/candidates", {\n        method: "POST",\n        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },\n        body: JSON.stringify({ recurringExpenseId: candidate.id, year: selectedPeriod.year, month: selectedPeriod.month, action }),\n      });\n      if (!response.ok) throw new Error("recurring_candidate_action_failed");\n      setRecurringRefresh((current) => current + 1);\n    } catch {\n      setRecurringError("定期支出を更新できませんでした。");\n    } finally {\n      setPendingRecurringId(null);\n    }\n  };\n\n'''
if anchor not in text: raise SystemExit('quick effect anchor missing')

(\n                    <>\n                      <button className="chip chip--active" type="button" disabled={pendingRecurringId === candidate.id || isPeriodLocked} onClick={() => void handleRecurringCandidate(candidate, "confirm")}>確定</button>\n                      <button className="chip" type="button" disabled={pendingRecurringId === candidate.id || isPeriodLocked} onClick={() => void handleRecurringCandidate(candidate, "skip")}>スキップ</button>\n                    </>\n                  ) : <span className="pill pill--muted">{candidate.status === "confirmed" ? "支出へ反映済み" : "今月はスキップ"}</span>}\n                </div>\n              </article>\n            );\n          })}\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </section>\n  );\n\n'''

from pathlib import Path
migration = "alter table recurring_expenses add column if not exists name text;\nupdate recurring_expenses re set name = c.name from categories c where c.id = re.category_id and (re.name is null or trim(re.name) = '');\nalter table recurring_expenses alter column name set not null;\n"
Path('postgres/migrations/007_add_recurring_expense_name.sql').write_text(migration)
Path('supabase/migrations/202606020003_add_recurring_expense_name.sql').write_text(migration.replace('alter table recurring_expenses', 'alter table public.recurring_expenses').replace('update recurring_expenses', 'update public.recurring_expenses').replace('from categories', 'from public.categories'))
prepare=Path('postgres/migrations/002_prepare_local_environments.sh'); text=prepare.read_text()
text=text.replace('recurring_notification_migration_file="/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/006_add_recurring_expense_notification.sql"','recurring_notification_migration_file="/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/006_add_recurring_expense_notification.sql"\nrecurring_name_migration_file="/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/007_add_recurring_expense_name.sql"')
text=text.replace('''  psql -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 --username "$POSTGRES_USER" --dbname "$database_name" \\
    -f "$recurring_notification_migration_file"''','''  psql -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 --username "$POSTGRES_USER" --dbname "$database_name" \\
    -f "$recurring_notification_migration_file"\n  psql -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 --username "$POSTGRES_USER" --dbname "$database_name" \\
    -f "$recurring_name_migration_file"''')
prepare.write_text(text)

seed=Path('postgres/migrations/003_seed_local_data.sql'); text=seed.read_text()
text=text.replace('insert into recurring_expenses (id, household_id, category_id, payment_source_id, paid_by_member_id, amount, day_of_month, scope, split_type, active)', 'insert into recurring_expenses (id, household_id, category_id, payment_source_id, paid_by_member_id, name, amount, day_of_month, scope, split_type, active)')
text=text.replace("    'aaaaaaaa-1111-4111-8111-aaaaaaaa1111',\n    1490,", "    'aaaaaaaa-1111-4111-8111-aaaaaaaa1111',\n    '動画サブスク',\n    1490,")
text=text.replace('''  paid_by_member_id = excluded.paid_by_member_id,
  amount = excluded.amount,''','''  paid_by_member_id = excluded.paid_by_member_id,
  name = excluded.name,
  amount = excluded.amount,''',1)
text=text.replace("and e.memo = c.name || ' 定期支出'", "and e.memo = re.name || ' 定期支出'")
seed.write_text(text)

api=Path('src/app/api/recurring-expenses/candidates/route.ts'); text=api.read_text()
text=text.replace('c.name as title, c.name as category', 're.name as title, c.name as category')
text=text.replace('re.split_type::text, c.name as title', 're.split_type::text, re.name as title')
api.write_text(text)

Path('src/app/api/recurring-expenses/route.ts').write_text('''import { NextResponse } from "next/server.js";\nimport { z } from "zod";\nimport { db } from "../../../lib/db.ts";\n\nexport const runtime = "nodejs";\nexport const dynamic = "force-dynamic";\nconst schema = z.object({ name: z.string().trim().min(1).max(100), category: z.string().trim().min(1).max(50), paidBy: z.enum(["a", "b"]), amount: z.number().int().positive(), dayOfMonth: z.number().int().min(1).max(31), scope: z.enum(["shared", "personal", "review"]), notificationEnabled: z.boolean().default(true) });\nconst householdQuery = `select id from households order by created_at asc limit 1`;\nconst membersQuery = `select id from household_members where household_id = $1 order by role asc, created_at asc`;\nconst categoryQuery = `insert into categories (household_id, name, kind, default_expense_scope, default_split_type) values ($1, $2, 'other', $3, 'equal') on conflict (household_id, name) do update set name = excluded.name returning id`;\nconst paymentQuery = `select id from payment_sources where household_id = $1 and owner_member_id = $2 and type = 'member_personal' order by created_at asc limit 1`;\nconst insertQuery = `insert into recurring_expenses (household_id, category_id, payment_source_id, paid_by_member_id, name, amount, day_of_month, scope, split_type, notification_enabled) values ($1,$2,$3,$4,$5,$6,$7,$8,'equal',$9) returning id::text`;\nexport async function POST(request: Request) {\n  const pool = db(); const client = await pool.connect();\n  try {\n    const parsed = schema.safeParse(await request.json()); if (!parsed.success) return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_recurring_expense" }, { status: 400 });\n    await client.query("begin"); const household=(await client.query<{id:string}>(householdQuery)).rows[0]; if(!household){await client.query("rollback");return NextResponse.json({error:"household_not_found"},{status:404});}\n    const members=(await client.query<{id:string}>(membersQuery,[household.id])).rows; const payer=parsed.data.paidBy==="a"?members[0]:members[1]; if(!payer){await client.query("rollback");return NextResponse.json({error:"payer_not_found"},{status:400});}\n    const category=(await client.query<{id:string}>(categoryQuery,[household.id,parsed.data.category,parsed.data.scope])).rows[0]; const payment=(await client.query<{id:string}>(paymentQuery,[household.id,payer.id])).rows[0]; if(!category||!payment){await client.query("rollback");return NextResponse.json({error:"recurring_source_not_found"},{status:400});}\n    const recurring=(await client.query<{id:string}>(insertQuery,[household.id,category.id,payment.id,payer.id,parsed.data.name,parsed.data.amount,parsed.data.dayOfMonth,parsed.data.scope,parsed.data.notificationEnabled])).rows[0]; await client.query("commit"); console.info("Created recurring expense",{id:recurring.id}); return NextResponse.json({id:recurring.id},{status:201});\n  } catch(error){await client.query("rollback");console.error("Failed to create recurring expense",error);return NextResponse.json({error:"recurring_expense_create_failed"},{status:500});} finally {client.release();}\n}\n''')
PY

addition='''  const createRecurringExpense = async () => {\n    const amount = Number(recurringDraft.amount);\n    const dayOfMonth = Number(recurringDraft.dayOfMonth);\n    if (!recurringDraft.name.trim() || !recurringDraft.category.trim() || !Number.isInteger(amount) || amount <= 0 || !Number.isInteger(dayOfMonth) || dayOfMonth < 1 || dayOfMonth > 31) {\n      setRecurringError("定期支出の入力内容を確認してください。");\n      return;\n    }\n    setRecurringError(null);\n    try {\n      const response = await fetch("/api/recurring-expenses", { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ ...recurringDraft, amount, dayOfMonth }) });\n      if (!response.ok) throw new Error("recurring_expense_create_failed");\n      setRecurringDraft({ name: "", category: "", paidBy: "a", amount: "", dayOfMonth: "10", scope: "shared", notificationEnabled: true });\n      setRecurringRefresh((current) => current + 1);\n    } catch { setRecurringError("定期支出を登録できませんでした。"); }\n  };\n\n'''
if anchor not in text: raise SystemExit('handler anchor missing')
text=text.replace(anchor,addition+anchor)
anchor='''      <div className="settings-grid">\n        <article className="panel settings-panel">'''
new='''      <div className="settings-grid">\n        <article className="panel settings-panel recurring-settings">\n          <h3>定期支出を登録</h3>\n          <div className="recurring-settings__grid">\n            <input aria-label="定期支出名" placeholder="例: 動画サブスク" value={recurringDraft.name} onChange={(event) => setRecurringDraft({ ...recurringDraft, name: event.target.value })} />\n            <input aria-label="定期支出カテゴリ" placeholder="例: サブスク" value={recurringDraft.category} onChange={(event) => setRecurringDraft({ ...recurringDraft, category: event.target.value })} />\n            <input aria-label="定期支出金額" inputMode="numeric" placeholder="金額" value={recurringDraft.amount} onChange={(event) => setRecurringDraft({ ...recurringDraft, amount: event.target.value })} />\n            <input aria-label="定期支出予定日" inputMode="numeric" placeholder="日" value={recurringDraft.dayOfMonth} onChange={(event) => setRecurringDraft({ ...recurringDraft, dayOfMonth: event.target.value })} />\n            <select aria-label="定期支出支払者" value={recurringDraft.paidBy} onChange={(event) => setRecurringDraft({ ...recurringDraft, paidBy: event.target.value as MemberId })}>{members.map((member) => <option key={member.id} value={member.id}>{member.name}</option>)}</select>\n            <select aria-label="定期支出分類" value={recurringDraft.scope} onChange={(event) => setRecurringDraft({ ...recurringDraft, scope: event.target.value as ExpenseScope })}><option value="shared">共通費</option><option value="personal">個人費</option><option value="review">要確認</option></select>\n            <button className="chip chip--active" type="button" onClick={() => void createRecurringExpense()}>定期支出を追加</button>\n          </div>\n        </article>\n        <article className="panel settings-panel">'''
if anchor not in text: raise SystemExit('settings grid anchor missing')
text=text.replace(anchor,new)

from pathlib import Path

product = Path('Docs/product-spec.md')
text = product.read_text()
text += '''\n## 実装済みMVP機能（2026-06-02）\n\n### 軽量な支出入力\n\n- 画面下部の入力欄から `店舗名 金額` 形式で登録する\n- 入力欄は通常時に畳み、フォーカス時だけ候補や補助操作を展開する\n- `仮登録` は入力内容を `要確認` として保存する\n- PWAショートカット `/?compose=quick` から入力欄へ直接フォーカスできる\n- 過去履歴から店舗名、カテゴリ、区分、直近金額を候補表示する\n\n### 支出整理と月次精算\n\n- 支出は `共通費`、`個人費`、`要確認` に分類する\n- 共通費の負担割合は `5:5`、`6:4`、`4:6`、`7:3`、`3:7` から選択する\n- 支出のタイトル、金額、日付、支払者、カテゴリ、メモを編集できる\n- 支出を削除できる\n- 精算済み月は支出の追加、編集、削除を禁止する\n- 精算取り消し時は理由と履歴を残し、再編集を許可する\n\n### 使用料グラフ\n\n- 選択月を含む直近6か月の支出推移を棒グラフで表示する\n- 選択月のカテゴリ別内訳を表示する\n- 区分、支払者、カテゴリで絞り込める\n- ローディング、エラー、空状態を表示する\n\n### 定期支出\n\n- 設定画面から名称、カテゴリ、支払者、標準金額、予定日、区分を登録する\n- 当月候補をホーム画面に表示する\n- 候補金額を修正して確定、または当月分をスキップする\n- 月ごとの処理記録により二重登録を防止する\n- 過去の確定額があれば前回差分を表示する\n- 引き落とし予定日までの日数を表示する\n- テンプレート単位で通知ON/OFFを保存する\n\n## MVPの未実装境界\n\n- 認証、世帯招待、世帯単位のアクセス制御は未実装\n- 通知ON/OFFは保存できるが、Push通知やメール配信は未実装\n- レシートOCR、明細分割、外部金融連携は未実装\n- 共通口座への入金管理は未実装\n'''
product.write_text(text)

screens = Path('Docs/screens.md')
text = screens.read_text()
text += '''\n## 現在の画面構成（2026-06-02）\n\n### 共通ヘッダー\n\n- 表示月の前月・翌月切替\n- レシートOCR用の将来導線\n- 今月の精算結果、共有費、要確認額\n\n### ホームタブ\n\n表示項目:\n\n- 使用料グラフ: 直近6か月、カテゴリ内訳、区分・支払者・カテゴリフィルタ\n- 今月の定期支出: 予定日、予定日までの日数、金額、前回差分、通知ON/OFF、確定、スキップ\n- 今月の流れ: 支出タイムライン\n- 要確認: 未整理支出と分類操作\n\n### 精算タブ\n\n- 月内支出の区分変更と負担割合変更\n- 要確認が0件の場合のみ精算確定\n- 精算済み月の取り消し\n- 確定・取り消し履歴\n\n### 履歴タブ\n\n- 支出の区分、負担割合確認\n- タイトル、金額、日付、支払者、カテゴリ、メモ編集\n- 支出削除\n\n### 設定タブ\n\n- 入力する人\n- 共通費の初期割合\n- 定期支出テンプレート登録\n\n### 固定composer\n\n- `店舗名 金額` 形式の入力\n- 店舗履歴候補\n- クイック入力\n- `仮登録` による要確認保存\n- PWAショートカット `/?compose=quick` からの直接フォーカス\n'''
screens.write_text(text)

model = Path('Docs/data-model.md')
text = model.read_text()
text = text.replace('''paid_by_member_id\namount\nday_of_month\nscope\nsplit_type\nactive''', '''paid_by_member_id\nname\namount\nday_of_month\nscope\nsplit_type\nactive\nnotification_enabled''')
text = text.replace('''## settlement_periods\n''', '''## recurring_expense_occurrences\n\n定期支出テンプレートの月ごとの処理状態。候補再取得時の二重登録を防止する。\n\n```txt\nid\nrecurring_expense_id\nyear\nmonth\nstatus\nexpense_id\namount\ncreated_at\nupdated_at\n```\n\nstatus:\n\n- confirmed\n- skipped\n\n## settlement_periods\n''')
text += '''\n## 追加マイグレーション\n\n- `202606010001_add_settlement_period_events.sql`: 精算確定・取り消し履歴\n- `202606020001_add_recurring_expense_occurrences.sql`: 定期支出の月次処理記録\n- `202606020002_add_recurring_expense_notification.sql`: 定期支出の通知ON/OFF\n- `202606020003_add_recurring_expense_name.sql`: 定期支出テンプレート名称\n'''
model.write_text(text)

api = Path('Docs/api-security-spec.md')
text = api.read_text()
text = text.replace('`"equal"` / `"a70"` / `"b70"`', '`"equal"` / `"a60"` / `"b60"` / `"a70"` / `"b70"`')
text += '''\n---\n\n## 5. 追加API（2026-06-02）\n\n### 5.1. 店舗候補 (`GET /api/expenses/suggestions`)\n\n過去の支出履歴から、入力途中の店舗名に合う候補を最大6件返す。店舗ごとによく使うカテゴリ、区分、直近金額を返す。\n\n| パラメータ | 型 | 必須 | ルール |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `q` | string | 任意 | 最大100文字 |\n\n### 5.2. 使用料グラフ (`GET /api/analytics`)\n\n選択月を含む直近6か月の支出推移、選択月のカテゴリ内訳、カテゴリ候補を返す。\n\n| パラメータ | 型 | 必須 | ルール |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `year` | string | 必須 | 2000〜9999 |\n| `month` | string | 必須 | 1〜12 |\n| `scope` | string | 任意 | `all` / `shared` / `personal` / `review` |\n| `paidBy` | string | 任意 | `all` / `a` / `b` |\n| `category` | string | 任意 | 最大50文字 |\n\n### 5.3. 定期支出候補 (`GET /api/recurring-expenses/candidates`)\n\n指定月の有効な定期支出テンプレートと処理状態を返す。予定日は月末を超えないよう補正する。\n\n### 5.4. 定期支出候補の処理 (`POST /api/recurring-expenses/candidates`)\n\n`confirm` または `skip` をトランザクション内で処理する。`confirm` は支出と負担割合を作成し、月次処理記録を保存する。`recurring_expense_id + year + month` の一意制約により二重登録を拒否する。\n\n### 5.5. 定期支出の通知設定 (`PATCH /api/recurring-expenses/candidates`)\n\nテンプレート単位の `notificationEnabled` を更新する。通知配信基盤自体は未実装。\n\n### 5.6. 定期支出テンプレート登録 (`POST /api/recurring-expenses`)\n\n名称、カテゴリ、支払者、標準金額、予定日、区分、通知ON/OFFを登録する。カテゴリが未登録の場合は世帯内カテゴリを作成する。\n\n### 5.7. 精算取り消し (`POST /api/settlements/reopen`)\n\n精算済み月を明示的に開き直す。理由を必須とし、取り消し履歴を保存する。開き直し後は支出の追加、編集、削除を再び許可する。\n\n## 6. 精算済み月の保護\n\n- 精算済み月では支出の追加、編集、削除を拒否する\n- 定期支出候補の確定・スキップも拒否する\n- 再編集には `POST /api/settlements/reopen` による明示的な取り消しが必要\n'''
api.write_text(text)
PY

from pathlib import Path

Path('postgres/migrations/008_add_savings_goals.sql').write_text('''create table if not exists savings_goals (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  household_id uuid not null references households(id) on delete cascade,
  name text not null,
  target_amount integer not null,
  current_amount integer not null default 0,
  target_date date,
  featured boolean not null default false,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  constraint savings_goals_name_required check (nullif(trim(name), '') is not null),
  constraint savings_goals_target_amount_positive check (target_amount > 0),
  constraint savings_goals_current_amount_non_negative check (current_amount >= 0)
);

create index if not exists savings_goals_household_id_idx
  on savings_goals(household_id, featured desc, target_date asc nulls last, created_at asc);
''')
Path('supabase/migrations/202606020004_add_savings_goals.sql').write_text(Path('postgres/migrations/008_add_savings_goals.sql').read_text())

p = Path('postgres/migrations/002_prepare_local_environments.sh')
s = p.read_text()
s = s.replace('recurring_name_migration_file="/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/007_add_recurring_expense_name.sql"\n', 'recurring_name_migration_file="/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/007_add_recurring_expense_name.sql"\nsavings_goals_migration_file="/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/008_add_savings_goals.sql"\n')
s = s.replace('  psql -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 --username "$POSTGRES_USER" --dbname "$database_name" \\\n    -f "$recurring_name_migration_file"\n', '  psql -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 --username "$POSTGRES_USER" --dbname "$database_name" \\\n    -f "$recurring_name_migration_file"\n  psql -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 --username "$POSTGRES_USER" --dbname "$database_name" \\\n    -f "$savings_goals_migration_file"\n')
p.write_text(s)

p = Path('postgres/migrations/003_seed_local_data.sql')
s = p.read_text()
addition = '''\n\ninsert into savings_goals (id, household_id, name, target_amount, current_amount, target_date, featured)\nvalues (\n  '99999999-9999-4999-8999-999999999991',\n  'aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa',\n  '沖縄旅行',\n  200000,\n  116000,\n  (date_trunc('month', current_date) + interval '4 months - 1 day')::date,\n  true\n)\non conflict (id) do update\nset\n  name = excluded.name,\n  target_amount = excluded.target_amount,\n  current_amount = excluded.current_amount,\n  target_date = excluded.target_date,\n  featured = excluded.featured;\n'''
if '99999999-9999-4999-8999-999999999991' not in s:
    s += addition
p.write_text(s)

Path('src/app/api/savings-goals/route.ts').write_text('''import { NextResponse } from "next/server.js";
import { z } from "zod";
import { db } from "../../../lib/db.ts";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const createSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().trim().min(1).max(100),
  targetAmount: z.number().int().positive(),
  targetDate: z.string().date().nullable().optional(),
  featured: z.boolean().default(false),
});
const contributionSchema = z.object({ savingsGoalId: z.string().uuid(), contributionAmount: z.number().int().positive() });
const householdQuery = `select id from households order by created_at asc limit 1`;
const goalsQuery = `select id::text, name, target_amount, current_amount, target_date::text, featured from savings_goals where household_id = $1 order by featured desc, target_date asc nulls last, created_at asc`;

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const pool = db();
    const household = (await pool.query<{ id: string }>(householdQuery)).rows[0];
    if (!household) return NextResponse.json({ goals: [] });
    return NextResponse.json({ goals: (await pool.query(goalsQuery, [household.id])).rows });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Failed to load savings goals", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "savings_goals_failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const pool = db();
  const client = await pool.connect();
  try {
    const parsed = createSchema.safeParse(await request.json());
    if (!parsed.success) return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_savings_goal" }, { status: 400 });
    await client.query("begin");
    const household = (await client.query<{ id: string }>(householdQuery)).rows[0];
    if (!household) { await client.query("rollback"); return NextResponse.json({ error: "household_not_found" }, { status: 404 }); }
    if (parsed.data.featured) await client.query("update savings_goals set featured = false where household_id = $1", [household.id]);
    const goal = (await client.query<{ id: string }>(`insert into savings_goals (household_id, name, target_amount, target_date, featured) values ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5) returning id::text`, [household.id, parsed.data.name, parsed.data.targetAmount, parsed.data.targetDate ?? null, parsed.data.featured])).rows[0];
    await client.query("commit");
    console.info("Created savings goal", { id: goal.id });
    return NextResponse.json({ id: goal.id }, { status: 201 });
  } catch (error) {
    await client.query("rollback");
    console.error("Failed to create savings goal", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "savings_goal_create_failed" }, { status: 500 });
  } finally { client.release(); }
}

export async function PATCH(request: Request) {
  const pool = db();
  const client = await pool.connect();
  try {
    const parsed = contributionSchema.safeParse(await request.json());
    if (!parsed.success) return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_savings_contribution" }, { status: 400 });
    await client.query("begin");
    const household = (await client.query<{ id: string }>(householdQuery)).rows[0];
    if (!household) { await client.query("rollback"); return NextResponse.json({ error: "household_not_found" }, { status: 404 }); }
    const goal = (await client.query<{ current_amount: number }>(`update savings_goals set current_amount = current_amount + $1, updated_at = now() where id = $2 and household_id = $3 returning current_amount`, [parsed.data.contributionAmount, parsed.data.savingsGoalId, household.id])).rows[0];
    if (!goal) { await client.query("rollback"); return NextResponse.json({ error: "savings_goal_not_found" }, { status: 404 }); }
    await client.query("commit");
    console.info("Added savings contribution", { savingsGoalId: parsed.data.savingsGoalId, contributionAmount: parsed.data.contributionAmount });
    return NextResponse.json({ currentAmount: goal.current_amount });
  } catch (error) {
    await client.query("rollback");
    console.error("Failed to add savings contribution", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "savings_contribution_failed" }, { status: 500 });
  } finally { client.release(); }
}
''')
PY

}\n  };\n\n'''

const { chromium } = require('playwright');
(async () => {
  const browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true });
  const page = await browser.newPage();
  page.on('console', msg => console.log('console:', msg.type(), msg.text()));
  page.on('pageerror', error => console.log('pageerror:', error.message));
  page.on('response', response => { if (response.url().includes('/api/')) console.log('response:', response.status(), response.url()); });
  await page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:3023/', { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });
  await page.waitForTimeout(5000);
  console.log('featured:', await page.getByLabel('最優先の貯金目標').count());
  console.log('body:', (await page.locator('body').innerText()).slice(0, 1200));
  await browser.close();
})();

from pathlib import Path
sql = '''create table if not exists budget_goals (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  household_id uuid not null references households(id) on delete cascade,
  category_id uuid not null references categories(id) on delete cascade,
  monthly_limit integer not null,
  active boolean not null default true,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  unique (household_id, category_id),
  constraint budget_goals_monthly_limit_positive check (monthly_limit > 0)
);

create index if not exists budget_goals_household_id_idx
  on budget_goals(household_id, active, created_at asc);
'''
Path('postgres/migrations/009_add_budget_goals.sql').write_text(sql)
Path('supabase/migrations/202606020005_add_budget_goals.sql').write_text(sql)

p = Path('postgres/migrations/002_prepare_local_environments.sh')
s = p.read_text()
s = s.replace('savings_goals_migration_file="/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/008_add_savings_goals.sql"\n', 'savings_goals_migration_file="/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/008_add_savings_goals.sql"\nbudget_goals_migration_file="/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/009_add_budget_goals.sql"\n')
s = s.replace('  psql -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 --username "$POSTGRES_USER" --dbname "$database_name" \\\n    -f "$savings_goals_migration_file"\n', '  psql -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 --username "$POSTGRES_USER" --dbname "$database_name" \\\n    -f "$savings_goals_migration_file"\n  psql -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 --username "$POSTGRES_USER" --dbname "$database_name" \\\n    -f "$budget_goals_migration_file"\n')
p.write_text(s)

p = Path('postgres/migrations/003_seed_local_data.sql')
s = p.read_text()
addition = '''\n\ninsert into budget_goals (id, household_id, category_id, monthly_limit, active)\nvalues (\n  '99999999-9999-4999-8999-999999999992',\n  'aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa',\n  'dddddddd-dddd-4ddd-8ddd-ddddddddddd4',\n  20000,\n  true\n)\non conflict (id) do update\nset\n  category_id = excluded.category_id,\n  monthly_limit = excluded.monthly_limit,\n  active = excluded.active;\n'''
if '99999999-9999-4999-8999-999999999992' not in s:
    s += addition
p.write_text(s)

Path('src/app/api/budget-goals/route.ts').write_text('''import { NextResponse } from "next/server.js";
import { z } from "zod";
import { db } from "../../../lib/db.ts";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const querySchema = z.object({
  year: z.string().transform(Number).pipe(z.number().int().min(2000).max(9999)),
  month: z.string().transform(Number).pipe(z.number().int().min(1).max(12)),
});
const createSchema = z.object({ category: z.string().trim().min(1).max(50), monthlyLimit: z.number().int().positive() });
const householdQuery = `select id from households order by created_at asc limit 1`;
const goalsQuery = `
  select bg.id::text, c.name as category, bg.monthly_limit,
    coalesce(sum(e.amount), 0)::int as spent_amount,
    greatest(bg.monthly_limit - coalesce(sum(e.amount), 0), 0)::int as remaining_amount
  from budget_goals bg
  join categories c on c.id = bg.category_id
  left join expenses e on e.household_id = bg.household_id and e.category_id = bg.category_id
    and e.expense_date >= make_date($2, $3, 1)
    and e.expense_date < make_date($2, $3, 1) + interval '1 month'
  where bg.household_id = $1 and bg.active
  group by bg.id, c.name, bg.monthly_limit, bg.created_at
  order by bg.created_at asc
`;

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  try {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    const parsed = querySchema.safeParse({ year: url.searchParams.get("year") ?? "", month: url.searchParams.get("month") ?? "" });
    if (!parsed.success) return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_period" }, { status: 400 });
    const pool = db();
    const household = (await pool.query<{ id: string }>(householdQuery)).rows[0];
    if (!household) return NextResponse.json({ goals: [] });
    return NextResponse.json({ goals: (await pool.query(goalsQuery, [household.id, parsed.data.year, parsed.data.month])).rows });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Failed to load budget goals", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "budget_goals_failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const pool = db();
  const client = await pool.connect();
  try {
    const parsed = createSchema.safeParse(await request.json().catch(() => null));
    if (!parsed.success) return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_budget_goal" }, { status: 400 });
    await client.query("begin");
    const household = (await client.query<{ id: string }>(householdQuery)).rows[0];
    if (!household) { await client.query("rollback"); return NextResponse.json({ error: "household_not_found" }, { status: 404 }); }
    const category = (await client.query<{ id: string }>(`insert into categories (household_id, name, kind, default_expense_scope, default_split_type) values ($1, $2, 'other', 'shared', 'equal') on conflict (household_id, name) do update set name = excluded.name returning id`, [household.id, parsed.data.category])).rows[0];
    const goal = (await client.query<{ id: string }>(`insert into budget_goals (household_id, category_id, monthly_limit) values ($1, $2, $3) on conflict (household_id, category_id) do update set monthly_limit = excluded.monthly_limit, active = true, updated_at = now() returning id::text`, [household.id, category.id, parsed.data.monthlyLimit])).rows[0];
    await client.query("commit");
    console.info("Created budget goal", { id: goal.id });
    return NextResponse.json({ id: goal.id }, { status: 201 });
  } catch (error) {
    await client.query("rollback");
    console.error("Failed to create budget goal", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "budget_goal_create_failed" }, { status: 500 });
  } finally { client.release(); }
}
''')
PY

replace('''  const [savingsContributions, setSavingsContributions] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});\n  const composerInputRef''', '''  const [savingsContributions, setSavingsContributions] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});\n  const [budgetGoals, setBudgetGoals] = useState<BudgetGoal[]>([]);\n  const [isBudgetGoalsLoading, setIsBudgetGoalsLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [budgetGoalError, setBudgetGoalError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [budgetGoalRefresh, setBudgetGoalRefresh] = useState(0);\n  const [budgetGoalDraft, setBudgetGoalDraft] = useState({ category: "", monthlyLimit: "" });\n  const composerInputRef''')
anchor = '''  const createSavingsGoal = async () => {\n'''
addition = '''  useEffect(() => {\n    let active = true;\n    setIsBudgetGoalsLoading(true);\n    setBudgetGoalError(null);\n    void fetch(`/api/budget-goals?year=${selectedPeriod.year}&month=${selectedPeriod.month}`)\n      .then((response) => { if (!response.ok) throw new Error("budget_goals_load_failed"); return response.json() as Promise<{ goals: BudgetGoal[] }>; })\n      .then((data) => { if (active) setBudgetGoals(data.goals); })\n      .catch(() => { if (active) { setBudgetGoals([]); setBudgetGoalError("節約目標を読み込めませんでした。"); } })\n      .finally(() => { if (active) setIsBudgetGoalsLoading(false); });\n    return () => { active = false; };\n  }, [selectedPeriod, budgetGoalRefresh, expenses]);\n\n  const createBudgetGoal = async () => {\n    const monthlyLimit = Number(budgetGoalDraft.monthlyLimit);\n    if (!budgetGoalDraft.category.trim() || !Number.isInteger(monthlyLimit) || monthlyLimit <= 0) {\n      setBudgetGoalError("カテゴリと月上限を確認してください。");\n      return;\n    }\n    setBudgetGoalError(null);\n    try {\n      const response = await fetch("/api/budget-goals", { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ ...budgetGoalDraft, monthlyLimit }) });\n      if (!response.ok) throw new Error("budget_goal_create_failed");\n      setBudgetGoalDraft({ category: "", monthlyLimit: "" });\n      setBudgetGoalRefresh((current) => current + 1);\n    } catch { setBudgetGoalError("節約目標を登録できませんでした。"); }\n  };\n\n'''
replace(anchor, addition + anchor)

"budget-goal-card budget-goal-card--over" : "budget-goal-card"}>\n                <div className="budget-goal-card__header"><div><span>{goal.category}</span><strong>{isOver ? `${formatMoney(goal.spent_amount - goal.monthly_limit)} 超過` : `残り ${formatMoney(goal.remaining_amount)}`}</strong></div><small>{formatMoney(goal.spent_amount)} / {formatMoney(goal.monthly_limit)}</small></div>\n                <div className="budget-goal-card__track"><span style={{ width: `${percentage}%` }} /></div>\n              </article>\n            );\n          })}\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </section>\n  );\n\n'''
replace(anchor, addition + anchor)
replace('''      {savingsGoalsPanel}\n\n      <div className="analytics-detail-grid">\n''', '''      {savingsGoalsPanel}\n      {budgetGoalsPanel}\n\n      <div className="analytics-detail-grid">\n''')

python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
Path('src/app/api/shared-wallet-contributions/route.ts').write_text('''import { NextResponse } from "next/server.js";
import { z } from "zod";
import { db } from "../../../lib/db.ts";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const querySchema = z.object({
  year: z.string().transform(Number).pipe(z.number().int().min(2000).max(9999)),
  month: z.string().transform(Number).pipe(z.number().int().min(1).max(12)),
});
const householdQuery = `select id from households order by created_at asc limit 1`;
const contributionsQuery = `
  select hm.display_name as member_name, coalesce(sum(swc.amount), 0)::int as amount
  from household_members hm
  left join shared_wallet_contributions swc
    on swc.household_id = hm.household_id and swc.member_id = hm.id
    and swc.contributed_at >= make_date($2, $3, 1)
    and swc.contributed_at < make_date($2, $3, 1) + interval '1 month'
  where hm.household_id = $1
  group by hm.id, hm.display_name, hm.role, hm.created_at
  order by hm.role asc, hm.created_at asc
`;

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  try {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    const parsed = querySchema.safeParse({ year: url.searchParams.get("year") ?? "", month: url.searchParams.get("month") ?? "" });
    if (!parsed.success) return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_period" }, { status: 400 });
    const pool = db();
    const household = (await pool.query<{ id: string }>(householdQuery)).rows[0];
    if (!household) return NextResponse.json({ total: 0, contributions: [] });
    const contributions = (await pool.query<{ member_name: string; amount: number }>(contributionsQuery, [household.id, parsed.data.year, parsed.data.month])).rows;
    return NextResponse.json({ total: contributions.reduce((total, item) => total + item.amount, 0), contributions });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Failed to load shared wallet contributions", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "shared_wallet_contributions_failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
''')

p = Path('src/components/kakeibo-dashboard.tsx')

replace('''  const [budgetGoalDraft, setBudgetGoalDraft] = useState({ category: "", monthlyLimit: "" });\n  const composerInputRef''', '''  const [budgetGoalDraft, setBudgetGoalDraft] = useState({ category: "", monthlyLimit: "" });\n  const [sharedWalletContributions, setSharedWalletContributions] = useState<SharedWalletContributions | null>(null);\n  const [isSharedWalletLoading, setIsSharedWalletLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [sharedWalletError, setSharedWalletError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const composerInputRef''')
anchor = '''  const createBudgetGoal = async () => {\n'''
addition = '''  useEffect(() => {\n    let active = true;\n    setIsSharedWalletLoading(true);\n    setSharedWalletError(null);\n    void fetch(`/api/shared-wallet-contributions?year=${selectedPeriod.year}&month=${selectedPeriod.month}`)\n      .then((response) => { if (!response.ok) throw new Error("shared_wallet_contributions_load_failed"); return response.json() as Promise<SharedWalletContributions>; })\n      .then((data) => { if (active) setSharedWalletContributions(data); })\n      .catch(() => { if (active) { setSharedWalletContributions(null); setSharedWalletError("共通口座への入金を読み込めませんでした。"); } })\n      .finally(() => { if (active) setIsSharedWalletLoading(false); });\n    return () => { active = false; };\n  }, [selectedPeriod]);\n\n'''
replace(anchor, addition + anchor)
anchor = '''  const savingsGoalsPanel = (\n'''
addition = '''  const sharedWalletPanel = (\n    <section className="section shared-wallet-panel" aria-label="共通口座への入金">\n      <div className="section__header">\n        <div>\n          <h2 className="section__title">共通口座への入金</h2>\n          <p className="section__meta">今月の生活費として入れた金額を確認する</p>\n        </div>\n        {sharedWalletContributions ? <strong>{formatMoney(sharedWalletContributions.total)}</strong> : null}\n      </div>\n      {isSharedWalletLoading ? <p className="analytics-empty">読み込み中...</p> : sharedWalletError ? <p className="analytics-empty">{sharedWalletError}</p> : (sharedWalletContributions?.contributions.length ?? 0) === 0 ? <p className="analytics-empty">今月の入金はまだありません。</p> : (\n        <div className="shared-wallet-list">\n          {sharedWalletContributions?.contributions.map((contribution) => (\n            <div key={contribution.member_name} className="shared-wallet-item"><span>{contribution.member_name}</span><strong>{formatMoney(contribution.amount)}</strong></div>\n          ))}\n        </div>\n      )}\n      <p className="shared-wallet-panel__note">収入額は表示せず、共通口座への入金額だけを共有します。</p>\n    </section>\n  );\n\n'''
replace(anchor, addition + anchor)
replace('''      {savingsGoalsPanel}\n      {budgetGoalsPanel}\n\n      <div className="analytics-detail-grid">\n''', '''      {savingsGoalsPanel}\n      {budgetGoalsPanel}\n      {sharedWalletPanel}\n\n      <div className="analytics-detail-grid">\n''')
p.write_text(s)

(async () => {
  const browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true });
  const page = await browser.newPage();
  page.on('pageerror', error => console.log('pageerror:', error.message));
  await page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:3023/', { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded', timeout: 30000 });
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '分析', exact: true }).click();
  const panel = page.getByLabel('共通口座への入金');
  await panel.waitFor({ state: 'visible' });
  const text = await panel.innerText();
  for (const expected of ['¥55,000', '葵', '¥30,000', '蓮', '¥25,000']) {
    if (!text.includes(expected)) throw new Error(`missing ${expected}: ${text}`);
  }
  console.log(text);
  await browser.close();
})();

from pathlib import Path
sql = '''create table if not exists shared_wallet_monthly_plans (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  household_id uuid not null references households(id) on delete cascade,
  year integer not null,
  month integer not null,
  target_amount integer not null,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  unique (household_id, year, month),
  constraint shared_wallet_monthly_plans_month_range check (month between 1 and 12),
  constraint shared_wallet_monthly_plans_target_positive check (target_amount > 0)
);

create index if not exists shared_wallet_monthly_plans_household_id_idx
  on shared_wallet_monthly_plans(household_id, year, month);
'''
Path('postgres/migrations/010_add_shared_wallet_monthly_plans.sql').write_text(sql)
Path('supabase/migrations/202606020006_add_shared_wallet_monthly_plans.sql').write_text(sql)

s=s.replace('  psql -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 --username "$POSTGRES_USER" --dbname "$database_name" \\\n    -f "$budget_goals_migration_file"\n','  psql -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 --username "$POSTGRES_USER" --dbname "$database_name" \\\n    -f "$budget_goals_migration_file"\n  psql -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 --username "$POSTGRES_USER" --dbname "$database_name" \\\n    -f "$shared_wallet_plans_migration_file"\n')
p.write_text(s)

p=Path('postgres/migrations/003_seed_local_data.sql')
s=p.read_text()
addition='''\n\ninsert into shared_wallet_monthly_plans (id, household_id, year, month, target_amount)\nvalues (\n  '99999999-9999-4999-8999-999999999993',\n  'aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa',\n  extract(year from current_date)::int,\n  extract(month from current_date)::int,\n  100000\n)\non conflict (household_id, year, month) do update\nset target_amount = excluded.target_amount;\n'''
if '99999999-9999-4999-8999-999999999993' not in s: s += addition
p.write_text(s)

p=Path('src/app/api/shared-wallet-contributions/route.ts')
s=p.read_text()
s=s.replace('''const householdQuery = `select id from households order by created_at asc limit 1`;\nconst contributionsQuery = `\n  select hm.display_name as member_name, coalesce(sum(swc.amount), 0)::int as amount\n''','''const planSchema = z.object({ year: z.number().int().min(2000).max(9999), month: z.number().int().min(1).max(12), targetAmount: z.number().int().positive() });\nconst householdQuery = `select id from households order by created_at asc limit 1`;\nconst planQuery = `select target_amount from shared_wallet_monthly_plans where household_id = $1 and year = $2 and month = $3`;\nconst contributionsQuery = `\n  select hm.display_name as member_name, hm.default_share_rate::float8 as share_rate, coalesce(sum(swc.amount), 0)::int as amount\n''')
s=s.replace('''    const contributions = (await pool.query<{ member_name: string; amount: number }>(contributionsQuery, [household.id, parsed.data.year, parsed.data.month])).rows;\n    return NextResponse.json({ total: contributions.reduce((total, item) => total + item.amount, 0), contributions });\n''','''    const contributions = (await pool.query<{ member_name: string; share_rate: number | null; amount: number }>(contributionsQuery, [household.id, parsed.data.year, parsed.data.month])).rows;\n    const targetAmount = (await pool.query<{ target_amount: number }>(planQuery, [household.id, parsed.data.year, parsed.data.month])).rows[0]?.target_amount ?? null;\n    const totalShareRate = contributions.reduce((total, item) => total + (item.share_rate ?? 0), 0);\n    const contributionTotal = contributions.reduce((total, item) => total + item.amount, 0);\n    return NextResponse.json({\n      total: contributionTotal,\n      target_amount: targetAmount,\n      remaining_amount: targetAmount == null ? null : Math.max(targetAmount - contributionTotal, 0),\n      contributions: contributions.map((item) => {\n        const shareRate = totalShareRate > 0 ? (item.share_rate ?? 0) / totalShareRate : 1 / contributions.length;\n        const proposedAmount = targetAmount == null ? null : Math.round(targetAmount * shareRate);\n        return { member_name: item.member_name, amount: item.amount, share_rate: shareRate, proposed_amount: proposedAmount, remaining_amount: proposedAmount == null ? null : Math.max(proposedAmount - item.amount, 0) };\n      }),\n    });\n''')
s += '''\nexport async function POST(request: Request) {\n  const pool = db();\n  const client = await pool.connect();\n  try {\n    const parsed = planSchema.safeParse(await request.json().catch(() => null));\n    if (!parsed.success) return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_shared_wallet_plan" }, { status: 400 });\n    await client.query("begin");\n    const household = (await client.query<{ id: string }>(householdQuery)).rows[0];\n    if (!household) { await client.query("rollback"); return NextResponse.json({ error: "household_not_found" }, { status: 404 }); }\n    await client.query(`insert into shared_wallet_monthly_plans (household_id, year, month, target_amount) values ($1, $2, $3, $4) on conflict (household_id, year, month) do update set target_amount = excluded.target_amount, updated_at = now()`, [household.id, parsed.data.year, parsed.data.month, parsed.data.targetAmount]);\n    await client.query("commit");\n    console.info("Updated shared wallet monthly plan", parsed.data);\n    return NextResponse.json({ targetAmount: parsed.data.targetAmount });\n  } catch (error) {\n    await client.query("rollback");\n    console.error("Failed to update shared wallet monthly plan", error);\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: "shared_wallet_plan_failed" }, { status: 500 });\n  } finally { client.release(); }\n}\n'''
p.write_text(s)

node - <<'JS'
const { chromium } = require('playwright');
(async () => {
  const browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true });
  const page = await browser.newPage();
  page.on('pageerror', error => console.log('pageerror:', error.message));
  await page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:3023/', { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded', timeout: 30000 });
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '分析', exact: true }).click();
  const panel = page.getByLabel('共通口座への入金');
  await panel.waitFor({ state: 'visible' });
  let text = await panel.innerText();
  for (const expected of ['¥55,000', '目標 ¥100,000', '提案 ¥55,000', '提案 ¥45,000']) {
    if (!text.includes(expected)) throw new Error(`missing ${expected}: ${text}`);
  }
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '設定', exact: true }).click();
  await page.getByLabel('共通口座の入金目標額').fill('110000');
  await Promise.all([
    page.waitForResponse(response => response.url().endsWith('/api/shared-wallet-contributions') && response.request().method() === 'POST' && response.ok()),
    page.getByRole('button', { name: '目標額を更新', exact: true }).click(),
  ]);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '分析', exact: true }).click();
  const updatedPanel = page.getByLabel('共通口座への入金');
  await updatedPanel.waitFor({ state: 'visible' });
  text = await updatedPanel.innerText();
  for (const expected of ['目標 ¥110,000', '提案 ¥60,500', '提案 ¥49,500']) {
    if (!text.includes(expected)) throw new Error(`missing ${expected}: ${text}`);
  }
  console.log(text);
  await browser.close();
})();

from pathlib import Path

def replace(path, old, new):
    p = Path(path)
    text = p.read_text()
    if old not in text:
        raise SystemExit(f'missing pattern in {path}: {old[:100]!r}')
    p.write_text(text.replace(old, new, 1))

migration = '''create table if not exists shared_wallet_member_plans (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  household_id uuid not null references households(id) on delete cascade,
  member_id uuid not null,
  year integer not null,
  month integer not null,
  planned_amount integer not null,
  status text not null default 'pending',
  completed_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  unique (household_id, member_id, year, month),
  constraint shared_wallet_member_plans_member_same_household
    foreign key (member_id, household_id)
    references household_members(id, household_id)
    on delete cascade,
  constraint shared_wallet_member_plans_month_range check (month between 1 and 12),
  constraint shared_wallet_member_plans_amount_positive check (planned_amount > 0),
  constraint shared_wallet_member_plans_status_valid check (status in ('pending', 'completed'))
);

create index if not exists shared_wallet_member_plans_household_id_idx
  on shared_wallet_member_plans(household_id, year, month);
'''
Path('postgres/migrations/011_add_shared_wallet_member_plans.sql').write_text(migration)
Path('supabase/migrations/202606020007_add_shared_wallet_member_plans.sql').write_text(migration)

insert into shared_wallet_member_plans (id, household_id, member_id, year, month, planned_amount, status, completed_at)

    'aaaaaaaa-2222-4222-8222-aaaaaaaa2222',
    extract(year from current_date)::int,
    extract(month from current_date)::int,
    45000,
    'pending',
    null

on conflict (household_id, member_id, year, month) do update
set

'''

python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p = Path('src/app/api/shared-wallet-contributions/route.ts')
p.write_text('''import { NextResponse } from "next/server.js";
import { z } from "zod";
import { db } from "../../../lib/db.ts";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const querySchema = z.object({
  year: z.string().transform(Number).pipe(z.number().int().min(2000).max(9999)),
  month: z.string().transform(Number).pipe(z.number().int().min(1).max(12)),
});
const planSchema = z.object({ year: z.number().int().min(2000).max(9999), month: z.number().int().min(1).max(12), targetAmount: z.number().int().positive() });
const memberPlanSchema = z.object({ memberId: z.string().uuid(), year: z.number().int().min(2000).max(9999), month: z.number().int().min(1).max(12), status: z.enum(["pending", "completed"]) });
const householdQuery = `select id from households order by created_at asc limit 1`;
const planQuery = `select target_amount from shared_wallet_monthly_plans where household_id = $1 and year = $2 and month = $3`;
const contributionsQuery = `
  select hm.id::text as member_id, hm.display_name as member_name, hm.default_share_rate::float8 as share_rate, coalesce(sum(swc.amount), 0)::int as amount, swmp.planned_amount, coalesce(swmp.status, 'pending') as status
  from household_members hm
  left join shared_wallet_contributions swc
    on swc.household_id = hm.household_id and swc.member_id = hm.id
    and swc.contributed_at >= make_date($2, $3, 1)
    and swc.contributed_at < make_date($2, $3, 1) + interval '1 month'
  left join shared_wallet_member_plans swmp
    on swmp.household_id = hm.household_id and swmp.member_id = hm.id
    and swmp.year = $2 and swmp.month = $3
  where hm.household_id = $1
  group by hm.id, hm.display_name, hm.role, hm.created_at, swmp.planned_amount, swmp.status
  order by hm.role asc, hm.created_at asc
`;

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  try {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    const parsed = querySchema.safeParse({ year: url.searchParams.get("year") ?? "", month: url.searchParams.get("month") ?? "" });
    if (!parsed.success) return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_period" }, { status: 400 });
    const pool = db();
    const household = (await pool.query<{ id: string }>(householdQuery)).rows[0];
    if (!household) return NextResponse.json({ total: 0, contributions: [] });
    const contributions = (await pool.query<{ member_id: string; member_name: string; share_rate: number | null; amount: number; planned_amount: number | null; status: "pending" | "completed" }>(contributionsQuery, [household.id, parsed.data.year, parsed.data.month])).rows;
    const targetAmount = (await pool.query<{ target_amount: number }>(planQuery, [household.id, parsed.data.year, parsed.data.month])).rows[0]?.target_amount ?? null;
    const totalShareRate = contributions.reduce((total, item) => total + (item.share_rate ?? 0), 0);
    const contributionTotal = contributions.reduce((total, item) => total + item.amount, 0);
    return NextResponse.json({
      total: contributionTotal,
      target_amount: targetAmount,
      remaining_amount: targetAmount == null ? null : Math.max(targetAmount - contributionTotal, 0),
      contributions: contributions.map((item) => {
        const shareRate = totalShareRate > 0 ? (item.share_rate ?? 0) / totalShareRate : 1 / contributions.length;
        const proposedAmount = targetAmount == null ? null : Math.round(targetAmount * shareRate);
        const plannedAmount = item.planned_amount ?? proposedAmount;
        return { member_id: item.member_id, member_name: item.member_name, amount: item.amount, share_rate: shareRate, proposed_amount: proposedAmount, planned_amount: plannedAmount, remaining_amount: plannedAmount == null ? null : Math.max(plannedAmount - item.amount, 0), status: item.status };
      }),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Failed to load shared wallet contributions", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "shared_wallet_contributions_failed" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const pool = db();
  const client = await pool.connect();
  try {
    const parsed = planSchema.safeParse(await request.json().catch(() => null));
    if (!parsed.success) return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_shared_wallet_plan" }, { status: 400 });
    await client.query("begin");
    const household = (await client.query<{ id: string }>(householdQuery)).rows[0];
    if (!household) { await client.query("rollback"); return NextResponse.json({ error: "household_not_found" }, { status: 404 }); }
    await client.query(`insert into shared_wallet_monthly_plans (household_id, year, month, target_amount) values ($1, $2, $3, $4) on conflict (household_id, year, month) do update set target_amount = excluded.target_amount, updated_at = now()`, [household.id, parsed.data.year, parsed.data.month, parsed.data.targetAmount]);
    await client.query("commit");
    console.info("Updated shared wallet monthly plan", parsed.data);
    return NextResponse.json({ targetAmount: parsed.data.targetAmount });
  } catch (error) {
    await client.query("rollback");
    console.error("Failed to update shared wallet monthly plan", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "shared_wallet_plan_failed" }, { status: 500 });
  } finally { client.release(); }
}

export async function PATCH(request: Request) {
  const pool = db();
  const client = await pool.connect();
  try {
    const parsed = memberPlanSchema.safeParse(await request.json().catch(() => null));
    if (!parsed.success) return NextResponse.json({ error: "invalid_shared_wallet_member_plan" }, { status: 400 });
    await client.query("begin");
    const household = (await client.query<{ id: string }>(householdQuery)).rows[0];
    if (!household) { await client.query("rollback"); return NextResponse.json({ error: "household_not_found" }, { status: 404 }); }
    const member = (await client.query<{ share_rate: number; total_share_rate: number; target_amount: number }>(`
      select hm.default_share_rate::float8 as share_rate, rates.total_share_rate::float8, swmp.target_amount
      from household_members hm
      join shared_wallet_monthly_plans swmp on swmp.household_id = hm.household_id and swmp.year = $3 and swmp.month = $4
      cross join lateral (select coalesce(sum(default_share_rate), 0) as total_share_rate from household_members where household_id = $1) rates
      where hm.household_id = $1 and hm.id = $2
    `, [household.id, parsed.data.memberId, parsed.data.year, parsed.data.month])).rows[0];
    if (!member) { await client.query("rollback"); return NextResponse.json({ error: "shared_wallet_member_plan_not_found" }, { status: 404 }); }
    const plannedAmount = Math.round(member.target_amount * (member.total_share_rate > 0 ? member.share_rate / member.total_share_rate : 0));
    await client.query(`
      insert into shared_wallet_member_plans (household_id, member_id, year, month, planned_amount, status, completed_at)
      values ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, case when $6 = 'completed' then now() else null end)
      on conflict (household_id, member_id, year, month) do update
      set status = excluded.status, completed_at = excluded.completed_at, updated_at = now()
    `, [household.id, parsed.data.memberId, parsed.data.year, parsed.data.month, plannedAmount, parsed.data.status]);
    await client.query("commit");
    console.info("Updated shared wallet member plan status", parsed.data);
    return NextResponse.json({ memberId: parsed.data.memberId, status: parsed.data.status });
  } catch (error) {
    await client.query("rollback");
    console.error("Failed to update shared wallet member plan status", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "shared_wallet_member_plan_failed" }, { status: 500 });
  } finally { client.release(); }
}
''')

node - <<'JS'
const { chromium } = require('playwright');
(async () => {
  const browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true });
  const page = await browser.newPage();
  await page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:3023/', { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded', timeout: 30000 });
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '分析', exact: true }).click();
  const panel = page.getByLabel('共通口座への入金');
  await panel.waitFor({ state: 'visible' });
  let text = await panel.innerText();
  for (const expected of ['予定 ¥55,000', '予定 ¥45,000', '入金待ち']) if (!text.includes(expected)) throw new Error(`missing ${expected}: ${text}`);
  const firstPending = panel.getByRole('button', { name: '入金待ち', exact: true }).first();
  await Promise.all([
    page.waitForResponse(r => r.url().endsWith('/api/shared-wallet-contributions') && r.request().method() === 'PATCH' && r.ok()),
    firstPending.click(),
  ]);
  await panel.getByRole('button', { name: '入金済み', exact: true }).waitFor();
  const completed = panel.getByRole('button', { name: '入金済み', exact: true });
  await Promise.all([
    page.waitForResponse(r => r.url().endsWith('/api/shared-wallet-contributions') && r.request().method() === 'PATCH' && r.ok()),
    completed.click(),
  ]);
  await panel.getByRole('button', { name: '入金待ち', exact: true }).first().waitFor();
  console.log(await panel.innerText());
  await browser.close();
})();

from pathlib import Path

def replace(path, old, new):
    p = Path(path)
    text = p.read_text()
    if old not in text:
        raise SystemExit(f'missing pattern in {path}: {old[:120]!r}')
    p.write_text(text.replace(old, new, 1))

''',
'''const memberPlanSchema = z.object({ memberId: z.string().uuid(), year: z.number().int().min(2000).max(9999), month: z.number().int().min(1).max(12), status: z.enum(["pending", "completed"]).optional(), plannedAmount: z.number().int().positive().optional() }).refine((value) => value.status != null || value.plannedAmount != null);
''')

'''    const member = (await client.query<{ share_rate: number; total_share_rate: number; target_amount: number }>(`
      select hm.default_share_rate::float8 as share_rate, rates.total_share_rate::float8, swmp.target_amount
      from household_members hm
      join shared_wallet_monthly_plans swmp on swmp.household_id = hm.household_id and swmp.year = $3 and swmp.month = $4
      cross join lateral (select coalesce(sum(default_share_rate), 0) as total_share_rate from household_members where household_id = $1) rates
      where hm.household_id = $1 and hm.id = $2
    `, [household.id, parsed.data.memberId, parsed.data.year, parsed.data.month])).rows[0];
    if (!member) { await client.query("rollback"); return NextResponse.json({ error: "shared_wallet_member_plan_not_found" }, { status: 404 }); }
    const plannedAmount = Math.round(member.target_amount * (member.total_share_rate > 0 ? member.share_rate / member.total_share_rate : 0));
    await client.query(`
      insert into shared_wallet_member_plans (household_id, member_id, year, month, planned_amount, status, completed_at)
      values ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, case when $6 = 'completed' then now() else null end)
      on conflict (household_id, member_id, year, month) do update
      set status = excluded.status, completed_at = excluded.completed_at, updated_at = now()
    `, [household.id, parsed.data.memberId, parsed.data.year, parsed.data.month, plannedAmount, parsed.data.status]);
    await client.query("commit");
    console.info("Updated shared wallet member plan status", parsed.data);
    return NextResponse.json({ memberId: parsed.data.memberId, status: parsed.data.status });
''',
'''    const member = (await client.query<{ share_rate: number; total_share_rate: number; target_amount: number; planned_amount: number | null; status: "pending" | "completed" | null }>(`
      select hm.default_share_rate::float8 as share_rate, rates.total_share_rate::float8, monthly_plan.target_amount, member_plan.planned_amount, member_plan.status
      from household_members hm
      join shared_wallet_monthly_plans monthly_plan on monthly_plan.household_id = hm.household_id and monthly_plan.year = $3 and monthly_plan.month = $4
      cross join lateral (select coalesce(sum(default_share_rate), 0) as total_share_rate from household_members where household_id = $1) rates
      left join shared_wallet_member_plans member_plan on member_plan.household_id = hm.household_id and member_plan.member_id = hm.id and member_plan.year = $3 and member_plan.month = $4
      where hm.household_id = $1 and hm.id = $2
    `, [household.id, parsed.data.memberId, parsed.data.year, parsed.data.month])).rows[0];
    if (!member) { await client.query("rollback"); return NextResponse.json({ error: "shared_wallet_member_plan_not_found" }, { status: 404 }); }
    const proposedAmount = Math.round(member.target_amount * (member.total_share_rate > 0 ? member.share_rate / member.total_share_rate : 0));
    const plannedAmount = parsed.data.plannedAmount ?? member.planned_amount ?? proposedAmount;
    const status = parsed.data.status ?? member.status ?? "pending";
    await client.query(`
      insert into shared_wallet_member_plans (household_id, member_id, year, month, planned_amount, status, completed_at)
      values ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, case when $6 = 'completed' then now() else null end)
      on conflict (household_id, member_id, year, month) do update
      set planned_amount = excluded.planned_amount, status = excluded.status, completed_at = case when excluded.status = 'completed' then coalesce(shared_wallet_member_plans.completed_at, now()) else null end, updated_at = now()
    `, [household.id, parsed.data.memberId, parsed.data.year, parsed.data.month, plannedAmount, status]);
    await client.query("commit");
    console.info("Updated shared wallet member plan", parsed.data);
    return NextResponse.json({ memberId: parsed.data.memberId, plannedAmount, status });
''')

const { chromium } = require('playwright');
(async () => {
  const browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true });
  const page = await browser.newPage();
  await page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:3023/', { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded', timeout: 30000 });
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '設定', exact: true }).click();
  const input = page.getByLabel('葵の入金予定額');
  await input.waitFor({ timeout: 30000 });
  await input.fill('60000');
  await Promise.all([
    page.waitForResponse(r => r.url().endsWith('/api/shared-wallet-contributions') && r.request().method() === 'PATCH' && r.ok()),
    page.getByRole('button', { name: '葵の予定額を更新', exact: true }).click(),
  ]);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '分析', exact: true }).click();
  const panel = page.getByLabel('共通口座への入金');
  await panel.getByText('予定 ¥60,000', { exact: false }).waitFor({ timeout: 30000 });
  await Promise.all([
    page.waitForResponse(r => r.url().endsWith('/api/shared-wallet-contributions') && r.request().method() === 'PATCH' && r.ok()),
    panel.getByRole('button', { name: '入金待ち', exact: true }).first().click(),
  ]);
  await panel.getByRole('button', { name: '入金済み', exact: true }).waitFor();
  const text = await panel.innerText();
  if (!text.includes('予定 ¥60,000')) throw new Error(`adjustment not preserved: ${text}`);
  console.log(text);
  await browser.close();
})();

node - <<'JS'
const { chromium } = require('playwright');
(async () => {
  const browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true });
  const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1280, height: 900 } });
  const pageErrors = [];
  page.on('pageerror', error => pageErrors.push(error.message));
  await page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:3023/', { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded', timeout: 30000 });
  await page.getByText('今月の流れ', { exact: true }).waitFor({ timeout: 30000 });
  for (const tab of ['分析', '精算', '履歴', '設定', 'ホーム']) {
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: tab, exact: true }).click();
  }
  const styles = await page.evaluate(() => {
    const css = [...document.styleSheets].flatMap(sheet => {
      try { return [...sheet.cssRules].map(rule => rule.cssText); } catch { return []; }
    }).join('\n');
    const body = getComputedStyle(document.body);
    return {
      background: body.backgroundColor,
      gradients: /linear-gradient|radial-gradient/.test(css),
      blur: /backdrop-filter:\s*blur/.test(css),
      errors: [],
    };
  });
  if (styles.gradients || styles.blur || pageErrors.length) throw new Error(JSON.stringify({ styles, pageErrors }));
  console.log(JSON.stringify({ ...styles, pageErrors }));
  await browser.close();
})();

python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path

def replace(path, old, new):
    p = Path(path)
    text = p.read_text()
    if old not in text:
        raise SystemExit(f'missing pattern in {path}: {old[:140]!r}')
    p.write_text(text.replace(old, new, 1))

path = 'DESIGN.md'
replace(path,
'''### 🚨 厳禁ルール (Anti-AI-Aesthetics)
- **グラデーションの禁止**: `linear-gradient` や `radial-gradient` は背景・コンポーネントを問わず一切使用しない。
- **ぼかし・半透明（グラスモルフィズム）の禁止**: `backdrop-filter: blur(...)` は完全禁止。背景色は常に不透明なソリッドカラーとする。
- **大きな影（ドロップシャドウ）の禁止**: `box-shadow` は原則使用しない。領域の区切りはすべて「枠線（ボーダー）」で行う。
- **過剰な角丸の禁止**: ボタンやカードの角丸（Radius）を `8px` や `12px` にしてカジュアルにしすぎない。Notion に近い `4px` 〜 `6px` のシャープな角丸に統一する。
''',
'''### AI感の定義

このプロジェクトでいう「AI感」とは、生成UIで頻出する、意味より装飾が先行した画面表現を指します。個々の装飾が単独で悪いのではなく、情報の優先順位や操作理由を説明できないまま積み重なる状態を避けます。

以下のいずれかに当てはまる場合は、AI感があるものとして修正対象にします。

- **装飾過多**: グラデーション、ぼかし、半透明、大きな影、過剰な角丸を使って見栄えだけを足している。
- **意味のない強調**: 状態や重要度が異なる要素を、すべて同じアクセントカラーや塗りボタンで強調している。
- **カードの乱立**: まとまりを考えず、すべての情報を同じ白いカードと枠線で分割している。
- **余白の不整合**: 同じ階層の要素で余白、入力欄、ボタン配置が揃わず、視線の流れが途切れる。
- **状態の曖昧さ**: hover、focus-visible、disabled、完了、警告などの違いが見た目で判断できない。
- **目的のない英語・コピー**: 画面理解に不要な英語見出し、抽象的なキャッチコピー、説明過多な文言を足している。

### 厳禁ルール (Anti-AI-Aesthetics)
- **グラデーションの禁止**: `linear-gradient` や `radial-gradient` は背景・コンポーネントを問わず一切使用しない。
- **ぼかし・半透明（グラスモルフィズム）の禁止**: `backdrop-filter: blur(...)` は完全禁止。背景色は常に不透明なソリッドカラーとする。
- **大きな影（ドロップシャドウ）の禁止**: `box-shadow` は原則使用しない。領域の区切りはすべて「枠線（ボーダー）」で行う。
- **過剰な角丸の禁止**: ボタンやカードの角丸（Radius）を `8px` や `12px` にしてカジュアルにしすぎない。Notion に近い `4px` 〜 `6px` のシャープな角丸に統一する。

### レビュー時の判定チェックリスト

- その色、枠線、背景は情報の意味を説明できるか。
- 主操作だけが強調され、状態表示は淡いセマンティックカラーに留まっているか。
- 同じ階層の入力欄とボタンが、同じリズムで並んでいるか。
- カードを減らしても情報構造が崩れない箇所に、不要なカードを足していないか。
- キーボード操作時に `focus-visible` が確認できるか。
- hover、disabled、完了、警告を色だけに頼らず文言でも判断できるか。
''')

const { chromium } = require('playwright');
(async () => {
  const browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true });
  const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1280, height: 900 } });
  const errors = [];
  page.on('pageerror', error => errors.push(error.message));
  await page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:3023/', { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded', timeout: 30000 });
  await page.getByText('今月の流れ', { exact: true }).waitFor({ timeout: 30000 });
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '精算', exact: true }).click();
  const shared = page.getByRole('button', { name: '共通費', exact: true }).first();
  const personal = page.getByRole('button', { name: '個人費', exact: true }).first();
  const sharedStyle = await shared.evaluate(el => ({ background: getComputedStyle(el).backgroundColor, color: getComputedStyle(el).color }));
  const personalStyle = await personal.evaluate(el => ({ background: getComputedStyle(el).backgroundColor, color: getComputedStyle(el).color }));
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '設定', exact: true }).click();
  const label = page.getByText('葵の予定額', { exact: true });
  await label.waitFor();
  const rowColumns = await label.locator('..').locator('div').evaluate(el => getComputedStyle(el).gridTemplateColumns);
  const targetInput = page.getByLabel('共通口座の入金目標額');
  await targetInput.focus();
  const focus = await targetInput.evaluate(el => ({ outline: getComputedStyle(el).outlineStyle, width: getComputedStyle(el).outlineWidth }));
  const css = await page.evaluate(() => [...document.styleSheets].flatMap(sheet => { try { return [...sheet.cssRules].map(rule => rule.cssText); } catch { return []; } }).join('\n'));
  const forbidden = /linear-gradient|radial-gradient|backdrop-filter:\s*blur/.test(css);
  if (errors.length || forbidden || focus.outline === 'none') throw new Error(JSON.stringify({ errors, forbidden, focus }));
  console.log(JSON.stringify({ sharedStyle, personalStyle, rowColumns, focus, errors, forbidden }));
  await browser.close();
})();

node - <<'JS'
const { chromium } = require('playwright');
(async () => {
  const browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true });
  const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1280, height: 900 } });
  const errors = [];
  page.on('pageerror', error => errors.push(error.message));
  await page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:3023/', { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded', timeout: 30000 });
  await page.getByText('今月の流れ', { exact: true }).waitFor({ timeout: 30000 });
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '精算', exact: true }).click();
  const firstRow = page.locator('.timeline-item').first();
  const styleOf = async name => firstRow.getByRole('button', { name, exact: true }).evaluate(el => ({ className: el.className, background: getComputedStyle(el).backgroundColor, color: getComputedStyle(el).color }));
  const scopes = { shared: await styleOf('共通費'), personal: await styleOf('個人費'), review: await styleOf('要確認') };
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '設定', exact: true }).click();
  const label = page.getByText('葵の予定額', { exact: true });
  await label.waitFor();
  const row = label.locator('..');
  const rowColumns = await row.locator('div').evaluate(el => getComputedStyle(el).gridTemplateColumns);
  const updateButton = row.getByRole('button', { name: '更新', exact: true });
  if (!await updateButton.isVisible()) throw new Error('member update button is not visible');
  const targetInput = page.getByLabel('共通口座の入金目標額');
  await targetInput.focus();
  const focus = await targetInput.evaluate(el => ({ outline: getComputedStyle(el).outlineStyle, width: getComputedStyle(el).outlineWidth }));
  const css = await page.evaluate(() => [...document.styleSheets].flatMap(sheet => { try { return [...sheet.cssRules].map(rule => rule.cssText); } catch { return []; } }).join('\n'));
  const forbidden = /linear-gradient|radial-gradient|backdrop-filter:\s*blur/.test(css);
  if (errors.length || forbidden || focus.outline === 'none') throw new Error(JSON.stringify({ errors, forbidden, focus }));
  console.log(JSON.stringify({ scopes, rowColumns, focus, errors, forbidden }));
  await browser.close();
})();

from pathlib import Path

def replace(path, old, new):
    p = Path(path)
    text = p.read_text()
    if old not in text:
        raise SystemExit(f'missing pattern in {path}: {old[:140]!r}')
    p.write_text(text.replace(old, new, 1))

  add column if not exists payday_day integer;

alter table household_members
  drop constraint if exists household_members_payday_day_range;

alter table household_members
  add constraint household_members_payday_day_range
  check (payday_day is null or payday_day between 1 and 31);
'''
